### [1] [The SU(N) Holstein Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25240)
*Chunhan Feng,Linh Pham,George Batrouni,Richard Scalettar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过行列式量子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究了SU(N) Holstein模型（N个费米子种类耦合到单个局域声子模式）。发现在半填充时存在低温绝缘电荷密度波（CDW）相，其中空位与N粒子位交替排列。确定了N=3时温度-电子-声子耦合相图，发现其临界温度Tc可达N=2时的两倍，并给出了Tc随N的变化关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷原子系统中存在SU(N)对称性（N>2），为了理解这类实验，需要研究SU(N) Hubbard模型。作者进一步将研究扩展到SU(N) Holstein模型，探索电子-声子耦合下的多费米子体系行为。

Method: 采用行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant Quantum Monte Carlo）模拟方法，研究SU(N) Holstein哈密顿量，其中N个费米子种类与单个局域声子模式耦合。

Result: 1) 在半填充时系统呈现低温绝缘CDW相，空位与N粒子位交替排列；2) 确定了N=3时在温度T-电子-声子耦合α平面上的CDW相图；3) N=3的临界温度Tc最高可达N=2时的两倍；4) 得到了固定ω₀和α下Tc随N的变化关系。

Conclusion: SU(N) Holstein模型在N>2时展现出比N=2更强的CDW有序，临界温度显著提高，这对理解冷原子系统中多分量费米子与声子耦合的实验具有重要意义。

Abstract: From the condensed matter physics perspective, the most natural single orbital tight-binding Hamiltonians, and hence the most widely studied, contain two fermionic species, corresponding to spin up and spin down electrons. In cold atom systems, however, SU(N) symmetry, in which $N > 2$ fermionic species reside within a single band, also occurs. In order to understand such experiments, the SU(N) Hubbard model has been increasingly studied. Here we present determinant Quantum Monte Carlo simulations of the SU(N) {\it Holstein} Hamiltonian, in which $N$ fermionic species couple to a single local phonon mode. We show that at half filling it has an insulating charge density wave phase (CDW) at low temperatures, in which empty sites alternate with sites with $N$ particles. We determine the $N=3$ CDW phase diagram in the temperature, $T$, versus electron-phonon coupling, $α$, plane at fixed phonon frequency $ω_0$ and half-filling $ρ=1.5$. The critical temperature $T_c$ for $N=3$ can be as high as twice the maximum attainable for $N=2$. We also obtain the $N$ dependence of $T_c$ for a representative, fixed, $ω_0$ and $α$.

</details>


### [2] [Intrinsic Magnetic Excitations and Heavy-Fermion Formation in the Frustrated Mn Pyrochlore System YMn$_{2+δ}$Zn$_{20-x}X_x$ ($X$ = In and Al) Revealed by Nuclear Magnetic Resonance and Nuclear Quadrupole Resonance Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25242)
*Shunsaku Kitagawa,Kenji Ishida,Yoshihiko Okamoto,Zenji Hiroi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过核磁共振和核四极共振测量，研究了d电子重费米子候选材料YMn₂₊δZn₂₀₋ₓInₓ和YMn₂₊δZn₂₀₋ₓAlₓ的微观电子态，发现In替代具有更高的位点选择性和更小的局域结构无序度，揭示了Mn烧绿石网络中的受阻磁激发与重费米子态的形成密切相关，而非传统的反铁磁量子临界涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究d电子重费米子候选材料中Mn烧绿石晶格磁涨落在重费米子形成中的作用，并厘清过量Mn原子对物理性质的复杂影响。

Method: 采用核磁共振（NMR）和核四极共振（NQR）测量，结合⁵⁵Mn-NQR的核自旋-晶格弛豫率1/T₁T的温度依赖性分析，以及In和Al替代的光谱对比。

Result: In替代的位点选择性远高于Al替代，引起的局域结构无序更小；低温下1/T₁T明显增强，表明低能激发随重费米子形成而发展，但其绝对值比YMn₂小约20倍，说明Mn-Mn间距增大显著削弱了磁相互作用。

Conclusion: 该体系的重费米子态源于Mn烧绿石网络，与受阻诱导的低能磁激发相关，而非传统的反铁磁量子临界涨落。

Abstract: We performed nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) and nuclear quadrupole resonance (NQR) measurements to investigate the microscopic electronic states of the $d$-electron heavy-fermion candidates $\mathrm{YMn_{2+δ}Zn_{20-x}In_x}$ and $\mathrm{YMn_{2+δ}Zn_{20-x}Al_x}$. In these compounds, magnetic fluctuations of the Mn pyrochlore lattice are expected to play an important role in heavy-fermion formation; however, excess Mn atoms complicate the interpretation of the physical properties. Our spectral analysis reveals that In substitution exhibits much higher site selectivity and introduces significantly less disorder in local structure than Al substitution. The temperature dependence of the nuclear spin-lattice relaxation rate divided by temperature $1/T_1T$ measured by $^{55}$Mn-NQR shows a clear enhancement at low temperatures, indicating the development of low-energy excitations associated with heavy-fermion formation. However, its absolute magnitude is approximately 20 times smaller than that in the related compound YMn$_2$, which hosts stronger antiferromagnetic correlations, indicating that the magnetic interactions are substantially weakened by the enlarged Mn-Mn distance. These results demonstrate that the heavy-fermion state in this system arises from the Mn pyrochlore network and is more closely associated with frustration-induced magnetic excitations with low energy than with conventional antiferromagnetic quantum-critical fluctuations.

</details>


### [3] [Kohn anomaly in a topological phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25381)
*Jingwen Li,Arpita Dutta,Kush Saha,Andrzej Szczerbakow,Tomasz Story,Manfred Fiebig,Shovon Pal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过温度依赖的太赫兹时域光谱，在Pb0.77Sn0.23Se中发现了跨越拓扑相变时低能表面声子模式的显著软化。这种软化不同于铁电体中的结构不稳定性，而是源于电子重构，归因于Kohn异常，表明拓扑相中晶格振动与狄拉克表面电子之间存在强耦合。声子线宽偏离标准非简谐温度依赖性进一步证实了增强的电声耦合。该结果建立了声子软化作为拓扑相变的光谱学标志，并提供了一种区分拓扑相与平庸相的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管电子层面的能带拓扑性质已被充分研究，但拓扑相变中晶格动力学和电声耦合的演化尚不清楚。本文旨在探索拓扑晶体绝缘体相变过程中声子模式的行为，并建立其与电子重构的关联。

Method: 使用温度依赖的时域太赫兹光谱技术，测量Pb0.77Sn0.23Se在拓扑相变过程中低能表面声子模式的频率和线宽变化。

Result: 观察到低能表面声子模式在拓扑相变点附近发生显著软化，且声子线宽偏离标准非简谐温度依赖性。该软化效应不伴随结构不稳定性，而是由电子重构导致的Kohn异常引起。

Conclusion: 声子软化的发现为拓扑相变提供了一种新的光谱学标志，并可用于区分拓扑相与平庸相。这表明电声耦合在拓扑相中显著增强，拓扑电子态与晶格振动之间存在强相互作用。

Abstract: Topological crystalline insulators extend the concept of topological insulators by hosting surface states protected by crystallographic symmetry. Their topological phase transitions arise from spin-orbit-driven band inversion in the bulk electronic structure, reshaping the low-energy electronic environment and its coupling to lattice excitations. While the electronic aspects of band topology are well established, the corresponding dynamics of lattice and electron-phonon interactions remain largely unexplored. Here, we report a pronounced softening of a low-energy surface phonon mode across the topological phase transition in Pb0.77Sn0.23Se, revealed by temperature-dependent time-domain terahertz spectroscopy. Unlike the well-known phonon softening in ferroelectrics, this effect does not signal a structural instability but instead reflects electronic reconstruction. We attribute the softening to the Kohn anomaly, indicating a strong coupling between lattice vibrations and Dirac-like surface electrons in the topological phase. Consistently, the phonon linewidth deviates from the standard anharmonic temperature dependence, further evidencing enhanced electron-phonon coupling. Our results establish phonon softening as a spectroscopic signature of topological phase transitions and provide a route to distinguish topological and trivial phases.

</details>


### [4] [Spin-imbalanced fermion on a dynamic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25411)
*Jie Liu,Xiaofan Zhou,Suotang Jia*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过密度矩阵重正化群模拟，研究了一维自旋-1/2费米子动力学晶格模型，发现两种不同的自旋密度波相，其序波矢分别对应自旋分辨费米面的嵌套矢量2k_{F↑}和2k_{F↓}，且对Hubbard相互作用具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一维关联系统中动力学晶格磁性的新颖调控途径，丰富对自旋-1/2费米子体系中局域自旋与巡游费米子耦合的微观理解。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）数值模拟，研究Ising型自旋依赖跳迁的一维自旋-1/2费米子动力学晶格模型，绘制基态相图。

Result: 获得顺磁相和自旋密度波相两种不同相；在部分自旋极化费米子相中，观察到自旋密度波序波矢分别对应2k_{F↑}和2k_{F↓}两种不同现象；且这两种自旋密度波相对巡游费米子间的排斥Hubbard相互作用具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该模型为一维关联系统中磁调制的调控提供了新途径，丰富了动力学晶格磁性的微观理解。

Abstract: We investigate the magnetic order of a one-dimensional spin-1/2 fermion dynamical lattice, where itinerant fermions are coupled to bond-centered localized spins via an Ising-like spin dependent hopping. The model provides an anisotropic dynamical extension of conventional spin-1/2 fermion systems, in which the motion of itinerant fermions is directly modulated by the configuration of localized spins. Using density matrix renormalization group simulations, we map out the ground state phase diagram in various parameter spaces. Depending on the interplay among the hopping dependent on localized spins, the longitudinal field, and the external Zeeman field, two distinct phases are obtained: a paramagnetic phase and a spin-density-wave phase. Most notably, in the partially spin-polarized fermion phase, the spin-density wave ordering wave vector exhibits two distinct phenomena, corresponding respectively to the nesting vectors $2k_{F\uparrow}$ and $2k_{F\downarrow}$ of the spin-resolved Fermi surfaces. We further demonstrate that the two spin-density wave phases are robust against the repulsive Hubbard interaction between itinerant fermions. Our results reveal a novel route for tuning magnetic modulations in one-dimensional correlated systems and enrich the microscopic understanding of dynamical lattice magnetism.

</details>


### [5] [Automatic-differentiation-enabled dynamic parameter retrieval with sub-pulse-width resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25479)
*Huaiyue Peng,Yuchen Lin,Fu Deng,Xiaoyue Zhou,Jingdi Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自动微分和全波形反演框架的太赫兹时域光谱分析方法，能够在亚脉冲宽度分辨率下从二维时域信号中可靠提取随时间变化的物理参数（散射率、等离子体频率、共振频率），解决了传统方法在超快时间尺度上光学响应函数失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间分辨太赫兹时域光谱在材料性质变化时间尺度与探测脉冲宽度相当或更短时，提取的光学电导率σ(ω,t_pp)无法可靠反映物质状态，掩盖了亚脉冲宽度内的动力学信息。

Method: 受频率分辨光学门控(FROG)多维信息检索思想启发，构建全波形反演框架，利用自动微分技术和二维时域信号E(t_g,t_pp)，通过梯度优化算法(Adam + L-BFGS)求解逆问题，在JAX框架下实现。

Result: 该方法实现了亚脉冲宽度分辨率的物理可观测量（时变散射率γ(t)、等离子体频率ω_p(t)和共振频率ω_0(t)）的可靠提取，对实验噪声和探测脉冲畸变具有优异的鲁棒性，并通过自洽数值方法和真实超快太赫兹光谱实验数据进行了验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的自动微分全波形反演方法为超快太赫兹光谱学提供了一种在响应函数失效条件下仍能可靠提取物理参数的新范式，拓展了太赫兹时域光谱在极端超快动力学研究中的应用能力。

Abstract: Time-resolved terahertz time-domain spectroscopy (THz-TDS) is a phase-sensitive tool in condensed matter physics for tracking photoinduced non-equilibrium dynamics of low-energy elementary excitations. However, the measured response function, optical conductivity $σ(ω,t_{pp})$, becomes unreliable in reporting the state of matter when material properties drastically change on a timescale comparable to or less than the probe pulse duration, obscuring the sub-pulse-width dynamics. To resolve this issue, we present a full-waveform inversion framework inspired by the multi-dimensional retrieval philosophy of frequency-resolved optical gating (FROG). By leveraging the automatic differentiation (AD) technique and the two-dimensional time-domain signal $E(t_{g},t_{pp})$, we show one can uniquely solve the inverse problem, at the sub-pulse-width resolution, of retrieving physical observables that are still well-defined, i.e., time-dependent scattering rate $γ(t)$, plasma frequency $ω_\mathrm{p}(t)$ and resonance frequency $ω_0(t)$, while the response functions are not. Further optimization by gradient-based routines (Adam + L-BFGS) via JAX makes the method exceptionally robust against experimental noise and probe pulse distortions. The validity of the AD-enabled methodology is benchmarked both by a self-consistent numerical approach and by experimental data from real ultrafast THz spectroscopy measurements.

</details>


### [6] [Fractional phase slips across the charge-density-wave domain walls in 1-T TiSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25963)
*Haotian Zhang,Zihao Song,Zhongchen Xu,Jun Shu,Zhongxu Wei,Zunming Lu,Jun Liu,Zengyi Du,Jinxing Zhang,Youguo Shi,Ge He,Jun Shen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过低温扫描隧道显微镜结合二维锁相分析，直接在实空间解析了1T-TiSe₂中电荷密度波（CDW）的局域相位结构。在均匀区域CDW相位均匀，而在畴壁处发现三个对称相关的CDW分量共同发生2π/3的相移。这一非平凡且关联的相位滑移结构对序参量流形施加了严格约束，挑战了纯粹的声子驱动公度锁相模型（该模型预测π相移）。最小自由能模型表明电子-声子和电子-空穴相互作用共同塑造了CDW的局域相位结构，揭示了电子相互作用的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1T-TiSe₂中电荷密度波的微观起源存在争议，主要有两种竞争机制：声子驱动的晶格不稳定性和电子驱动的激子关联。直接解析CDW的实空间相位结构可以为区分这些机制提供关键实验约束。

Method: 结合低温扫描隧道显微镜（STM）与二维锁相分析技术，在实空间直接解析CDW局域相位，并追踪其在畴壁上的演变。

Result: 均匀区域CDW相位均匀；畴壁处三个对称相关的CDW分量共同发生2π/3的相位滑移，而非简单声子模型预测的π相移。

Conclusion: CDW序的局域相位结构受电子相互作用显著影响，畴壁可作为多分量有序系统中微观相互作用的实空间探针。该相位分辨框架可推广用于约束关联材料中的竞争有序机制。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the charge density wave (CDW) in 1\textit{T}-TiSe$_2$ remains controversial, with competing scenarios based on phonon-driven lattice instability and electronically driven excitonic correlations. Here, we combine low-temperature scanning tunneling microscopy with two-dimensional lock-in phase analysis to directly resolve the local CDW phase in real space and track its evolution across individual domain walls. In homogeneous regions, the CDW phase remains uniform; by contrast, across domain walls we uncover a robust and reproducible $2π/3$ phase shift that occurs collectively in all three symmetry-related CDW components. This nontrivial and correlated phase-slip configuration places stringent constraints on the order-parameter manifold and challenges the simplest purely phonon-driven commensurate lock-in picture, which would instead predict a $π$ phase shift. A minimal free-energy model incorporating both electron-phonon and electron-hole interactions reproduces the observed phase behavior and indicates that electronic interactions play an important role in shaping the local phase structure of the CDW order. These results establish domain walls as direct real-space probes of the microscopic interactions underlying multicomponent order and provide a general phase-resolved framework for constraining competing ordering mechanisms in correlated materials.

</details>


### [7] [Spin modulations in the Rashba-Hubbard chain -- a tensor network study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26014)
*Jozef Genzor,Roman Krčmár,Andrej Gendiar,Chia-Min Chung,Denis Kochan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究开放一维Rashba-Hubbard链中可精确消除的自旋-轨道耦合效应，证明其在哈密顿量层面可通过SU(2)规范变换映射为普通Hubbard链，但在实验室系自旋关联中不可消除，并通过DMRG计算验证了填充依赖性自旋结构因子侧带等可观测后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究一维开放边界单带Hubbard链中均匀自旋-轨道耦合在哈密顿量层面可精确消除（SU(2)规范场），但在实验室系自旋关联中不可消除这一分离现象的物理后果。

Method: 利用密度矩阵重正化群（DMRG）计算，对具有排斥相互作用的开放边界一维Rashba-Hubbard链进行数值模拟，通过位点依赖的自旋旋转将模型映射为具有重正化跃迁的普通Hubbard链，并分析不同填充下的自旋结构因子。

Result: 电荷和能量诊断仅受带宽重正化影响（弱耦合下为二次效应）；自旋关联在线性阶即响应，自旋结构因子出现预测的自旋-轨道侧带：半填充时主导磁波矢k0=π变换为单一平面自旋螺旋波k=π-k_so；远离半填充时，非公度Hubbard自旋响应分裂为两个自旋-轨道偏移分量，产生实空间拍频图案。

Conclusion: 为精确可消除的一维自旋-轨道耦合极限提供了填充分辨的张量网络基准，并为梯子、多轨道链、环、邻近化导线及更高维Hubbard系统（其中自旋-轨道耦合不可规范消除）建立了可控参考点。

Abstract: Uniform spin-orbit coupling in an open single-band Hubbard chain is an exactly removable \(SU(2)\) gauge field at the Hamiltonian level, but not at the level of laboratory-frame spin correlations. We study this separation using density matrix renormalization group calculations for the repulsive one-dimensional Rashba-Hubbard chain. For open boundary conditions, a site-dependent spin rotation maps the model with hopping \(t\) and Rashba spin-orbit strength \(λ\) onto the ordinary Hubbard chain with renormalized hopping \(t_λ=\sqrt{t^2+λ^2}\). Consequently, charge and energy diagnostics are affected only through the bandwidth renormalization, which is quadratic in weak \(λ/t\). Spin correlations, however, respond already at linear order because the same transformation rotates the local spin basis by the wave vector \(k_{\rm so}=2\arctan(λ/t)\). We use DMRG to verify this observable consequence across the filling diagram of finite open chains. The filling structure follows the gauge-equivalent Hubbard model, whereas the spin structure factor shows the predicted spin-orbit sidebands. A dominant Hubbard-chain magnetic wave vector \(k_0\) is transformed into components at \(k_0\pm k_{\rm so}\), folded into the open-chain Brillouin zone. At half filling, where \(k_0=π\), the two sidebands fold onto a single, in-plane, spin spiral wave with \(k=π-k_{\rm so}<π\). Away from half filling, the incommensurate Hubbard spin response splits into two distinct spin-orbit-shifted components, producing a real-space beating pattern. Our results provide a filling-resolved tensor-network benchmark for the exactly removable limit of one-dimensional spin-orbit coupling, and establish a controlled reference point for ladders, multiorbital chains, rings, proximitized wires, and higher-dimensional Hubbard systems where spin-orbit coupling can no longer be gauged away.

</details>


### [8] [Folds of one curve: the superradiant phase diagram of Dicke modes with interacting matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26081)
*Max Hörmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文从热力学极限角度严格推导了Dicke模型的自由能泛函形式，揭示了超辐射相变由物质态方程的单连通曲线折叠而非不同分支交叉引起，并给出了多种磁性模型的精确相图与临界行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Dicke模型在热力学极限下的严格理论框架，阐明光子场与相互作用物质耦合的完整热力学结构，并统一描述超辐射相变的不同类型（连续、一级折叠、Larkin-Pikin型等）。

Method: 通过积分掉腔模得到磁化的严格自洽泛函；利用Landau理论进行级数展开；采用1/d展开、严格封锁iDMRG等数值方法分析具体模型。

Result: 发现超辐射一级相变是物质态方程单一连通曲线的折叠而非不同分支交叉；给出Dicke-Ising模型精确Landau系数和封闭形式的二级相变边界；揭示了Dicke-Heisenberg模型的精确三临界点等。

Conclusion: 光子场不创造物质本身不具有的相，超辐射相变完全由物质性质决定；不同磁性模型的相图结构可由统一框架理解。

Abstract: We give a thermodynamic-limit account of Dicke models with one cavity mode coupled collectively to interacting matter. Integrating out the cavity yields an exact self-consistent functional of the magnetisation $m$, $\tilde e(m) = λm^2/2 + e_{\rm mat}(λm)$: a classical penalty on the bare-matter energy $e_{\rm mat}$ in the self-consistent field $h = λm$, with $λ= g^2/(2ω_c)$ the collective coupling. Supplying only that scalar field, the photon creates no phase the matter does not already possess. States holding a minimum form one connected curve, $λ(m) = μ_{\rm mat}^{-1}(m)/m$, so superradiant first-order transitions are folds of one equation of state not crossings of disjoint sheets, and a fold can straighten into a continuous line. The remaining rules are local, each with a spectral counterpart: onset by the leading singularity of $e_{\rm mat}$ (a softening polariton), order by one bare response -- the Landau quartic, or a divergent susceptibility forcing a Larkin-Pikin (LP) fold. For the Dicke-Ising model the Landau coefficients are exact, giving in closed form the second-order boundary and both zero-quartic fields, one tricritical; a $1/d$ expansion maps all four phases, with the AS-PS transition first order for $d\le d_{uc}=3=4-z$ (LP) and tricritical points in the $(d,ε)$ plane above. At the degenerate quadruple point the matter is a Rydberg-blockade chain, solved by strict-blockade iDMRG: the antiferromagnetic superradiant (AS) phase persists as a finite 1D wedge, first order into the corner. Other magnets: the triangular antiferromagnet keeps a continuous superradiant-superradiant line (3D-XY, no fold forced); the compass chain a BKT-functional onset; the Heisenberg and XX chains, via a conserved operator, a spectrally silent first-order onset; and the Dicke-Heisenberg diagram an exact tricritical point at the saturation corner.

</details>

*Niclas Heinsdorf,Ken Shiozaki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过无限矩阵乘积态计算四维陈绝缘体的三维贝里曲率，证明了高阶贝里曲率可用于以显式量子化的方式计算第二陈数，并探讨了磁电耦合与高阶贝里相位的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上第二陈数的计算依赖于解析表达式或数值方法，本文旨在展示高阶贝里曲率（三维贝里曲率）可以作为一种替代方法，以显式量子化的方式计算第二陈数，并探索其与磁电耦合的联系。

Method: 将四维陈绝缘体的晶格模型重写为三维布里渊区上的平移不变无限链族，利用无限矩阵乘积态计算更高阶的三形式贝里曲率，进而计算DDKS数的拓扑相图。

Result: 计算得到的DDKS数相图与已知解析表达的第二陈数相图完全一致，证明了高阶贝里曲率可用于显式量子化地计算第二陈数。

Conclusion: 高阶贝里曲率是计算第二陈数的有效工具，并且与三维磁电耦合（轴子耦合）存在联系，可以通过高阶贝里相位来研究。

Abstract: We rewrite a lattice model of the four-dimensional Chern insulator as a family of translationally-invariant infinite chains over the three-dimensional Brillouin zone and compute its higher three-form Berry curvature using infinite matrix product states (iMPS). We calculate the topological phase diagram of the associated Dixmier--Douady--Kapustin--Spodyneiko (DDKS) number as a function of the model's mass term, and show that it is exactly congruent to the phase diagram in terms of the second Chern number, the analytic expression of which is known for this particular model. This agreement demonstrates that higher Berry curvature can be used to compute second Chern numbers in a manifestly quantized manner. Motivated by the connection between the second Chern form and the Chern--Simons axion coupling, we study magnetoelectric coupling in three dimensions and its relation to higher Berry phases.

</details>

### [10] [BFMTrack: Latent Sequence Optimization for Physics-Based Motion Tracking with Behavioral Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25056)
*Thomas Rupf,Agon Serifi,David Müller,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出潜序列优化（LSO）方法，通过策略梯度更新和时序相关噪声建模，扩展行为基础模型（BFMs）的能力，使其能够精确跟踪动态运动序列，无需奖励工程，并在密集跟踪、稀疏关键帧和真实人形机器人部署上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为基础模型（BFMs）在时间不变任务（如目标到达）上表现优异，但其潜空间不支持时变目标（如运动序列跟踪）。现有启发式方法（滑动窗口平均）无法捕捉高度动态运动的细微变化，且需要繁琐的奖励工程。

Method: 提出潜序列优化（LSO），结合仿真 rollout 与策略梯度更新，对潜变量序列进行优化。通过时序相关噪声（temporally correlated noise）对潜轨迹建模，引导优化过程产生平滑、连贯的潜序列。

Result: 在密集运动跟踪、稀疏关键帧插值以及真实人形机器人直接部署三个任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: LSO 无需奖励工程即可将 BFMs 的能力扩展到精确运动跟踪，生成的潜轨迹平滑连贯，且能够直接迁移到真实机器人上。

Abstract: Behavioral Foundation Models (BFMs) offer a promising path toward universal physics-based character control by organizing a rich repertoire of physically plausible behaviors into a latent space, guided by a large-scale motion dataset. While these models excel at time-invariant tasks, such as goal-reaching and state-based reward optimization, their latent space does not directly support time-varying objectives, such as tracking a motion sequence. For tracking, existing heuristics rely on moving-window-averaging that fails to capture the nuances of highly dynamic motions. In this work, we propose a novel Latent Sequence Optimization (LSO) to address these shortcomings. Our approach combines simulation rollouts with a policy gradient update to optimize over a sequence of latents, extending the capabilities of BFMs toward precise motion tracking without requiring reward engineering and tuning. To guide the optimization toward smooth, coherent latent trajectories, we model the latent sequence using temporally correlated noise. We validate our approach across dense tracking, sparse keyframing, and direct deployment onto a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [11] [Invariant Kalman filtering for extended pose estimation in multi-IMU articulated rigid-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25083)
*Sven Goffin,Cédric Schwartz,Silvère Bonnabel,Olivier Brüls,Pierre Sacré*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于相对L-扩展位姿的迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IterIEKF），用于IMU赋能的关节刚体系统位姿估计。该方法将运动链系统的位姿表示为李群形式，利用组仿射动力学特性，并以无噪声伪测量形式引入关节运动学约束。在UR5e机器人和人体腿部上的实验表明，该方法相比所有基线方法（EKF、IterEKF、绝对位姿IterIEKF）收敛更快、波动更小、RMSE降低至少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IEKF）仅适用于单个刚体，具有收敛保证和不可观测性下的一致性，但将其扩展到关节刚体系统面临挑战：刚体间位姿耦合导致无法直接应用，且如何在不变框架内融入关节运动学约束仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 提出相对L-扩展位姿（relative L-extended pose）这一李群表示，用于描述运动链系统。每个刚体配备一个IMU，得到组仿射动力学（group-affine dynamics），并将关节约束以不变形式表达为无噪声伪测量，融入迭代IEKF（IterIEKF）框架中。

Result: 在UR5e机器人和人体腿部上验证，所提出的IterIEKF在所有场景下均优于EKF、IterEKF和绝对位姿IterIEKF基线，收敛更快、运行间波动更小，RMSE至少降低50%。

Conclusion: 本文成功将不变滤波的收敛与一致性保证从单刚体扩展到了关节刚体系统，为IMU赋能的机器人运动估计和人体运动分析提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate extended pose estimation (orientation, velocity, and position) for IMU-instrumented articulated rigid-body systems is a key challenge in robotics and human motion analysis. The invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) addresses this problem for a single rigid body with convergence guarantees and consistency under unobservability, but extending these properties to articulated systems is nontrivial: inter-body pose coupling prevents a direct application, and incorporating joint kinematic constraints within the invariant framework remains an open problem. To address this gap, we introduce the relative L-extended pose, a Lie group representation for kinematic-tree systems. With one IMU per body, it yields group-affine dynamics and allows joint constraints to be expressed in invariant form. We incorporate these constraints as noise-free pseudo-measurements within an iterated IEKF (IterIEKF), thereby preserving the convergence and consistency guarantees of invariant filtering. Validated on both a UR5e robot and a human leg, the proposed IterIEKF outperforms all EKF, IterEKF, and absolute-pose IterIEKF baselines. It converges faster, exhibits lower run-to-run variability, and consistently achieves the lowest RMSE, with reductions of at least 50% compared to the second-best filter across all scenarios considered in this work.

</details>

*Hasan Moughnieh,Ibrahim Ghaddar,Hadi Elham,Imad H. Elhajj,Daniel Asmar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ADM-Fusion，一种基于深度学习的端到端多传感器融合方法，采用自适应传感器混合专家框架和内容感知路由，动态分配传感器权重，结合分离的平移/旋转分支和跨任务注意力机制，在仿真和真实数据集上展现出鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同传感器模态在不同退化环境中以不同方式失效，而现有融合方法依赖固定权重，无法自适应地平衡互补线索，尤其在自运动估计中需要动态调整传感器权重以保持鲁棒性。

Method: 提出ADM-Fusion：1) 自适应传感器混合专家框架（MoE）结合内容感知路由，实时动态分配传感器权重；2) 分离的平移和旋转分支，通过跨任务注意力机制实现任务特化与信息共享；3) 在CARLA-LOC仿真数据集上训练，并在KITTI真实数据集上微调，实现仿真到真实的迁移。

Conclusion: ADM-Fusion通过自适应传感器权重分配和任务特化分支设计，有效应对环境变化和传感器退化，为多传感器融合提供了一种鲁棒的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Robust multi-sensor fusion is essential for reliable autonomy in diverse and degraded environments, where sensor reliability can fluctuate rapidly. Because different modalities fail in distinct ways, effective fusion should adaptively balance complementary cues rather than rely on fixed weighting. This adaptability is particularly important for ego-motion estimation, since accurate updates depend on the consistent integration of complementary sensor information. We propose ADM-Fusion, an end-to-end deep learning based multi-sensor fusion method designed to adapt to environmental changes and sensor degradation. ADM-Fusion employs an adaptive sensor mixture-of-experts framework with content-aware routing to dynamically assign weights to sensor inputs in real time. The system further incorporates separate translation and rotation branches, coupled through a cross-task attention mechanism to preserve task-specific specialization while enabling information sharing. ADM-Fusion is trained on the CARLA-LOC simulated dataset and subsequently fine-tuned on KITTI real-world data, demonstrating effective simulation-to-real transfer. Experiments show that ADM-Fusion remains robust under degraded conditions while maintaining competitive performance against existing methods.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出SurveilNav协作导航框架，包含四个核心模块：主动相机调度、联合2D/3D建图、VLM（视觉语言模型）价值估计以及协作目标验证。该框架将机器人的动态局部感知与监控的静态全局视角协同起来。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上的实验表明，SurveilNav在探索效率和导航成功率上均显著优于现有方法，达到SOTA水平。此外，系统在大规模搜索、家庭环境及救援任务中展现出较强的应用潜力。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of surveillance systems in factories, offices, and homes, integrating them with robots offers a promising direction for collaborative and efficient task execution. However, existing approaches largely focus on single-robot scenarios and struggle with multi-view collaboration in large-scale environments. In this paper, we present a novel indoor collaborative object navigation dataset built on Habitat-Sim, featuring 206 cameras across 74 floors. The dataset enables systematic evaluation of an agent's ability to exploit multi-view surveillance information. To address the limitations of single-robot perception, we propose SurveilNav, a collaborative navigation framework that integrates active camera scheduling, joint 2D/3D mapping, VLM-based value estimation, and collaborative target verification. By synergizing the robot's dynamic local perception with the static global view of surveillance, this architecture effectively overcomes both the limited perception range of single agents and the inherent blind spots of fixed cameras, resolving inefficient exploration. Experimental results on the HM3D dataset demonstrate that SurveilNav substantially outperforms existing methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance in both exploration efficiency and navigation success rate. Moreover, the system shows strong potential for applications in large-scale search, home environments, and rescue missions.

</details>


### [14] [AeroCast: Probabilistic 3D Trajectory Prediction for Non-Cooperative Aerial Obstacles via Transformer-MDN Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25122)
*Syed Izzat Ullah,Jose Baca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AeroCast是一种结合Transformer编码器与混合密度网络的概率轨迹预测框架，用于非合作空中障碍物的未来位置预测，在五秒预测范围内将位移误差降低约50%，推理速度达0.1ms/样本，支持100Hz实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行器在共享空域中需要预测非合作障碍物（如鸟类、失控无人机、碎片）的未来位置以规划规避机动。非合作障碍物的运动呈现多模态特性，现有方法要么使用循环编码器难以捕捉长程运动前兆，要么仅产生点预测而无法提供分布信息给下游规划器。

Method: AeroCast采用Transformer编码器结合Mixture Density Network（MDN）输出头，逐时间步预测三维位移的高斯混合分布。创新点包括：平移不变的连续位移编码（translation-invariant consecutive displacement encoding）解决输入设计问题；面向校准的训练目标（calibration-oriented training objective）缓解混合模型中的模式退化问题。

Result: 在涵盖九种运动类别的混合真实/合成四旋翼数据集上，AeroCast在五秒预测范围内将ADE和FDE相对基线降低约50%，并在负对数似然和连续排名概率分数上取得最优。消融分析表明速度输入和模型容量是预测质量的主要贡献者，位置编码对长时域轨迹一致性至关重要。推理速度0.1ms/样本，支持100Hz实时部署。

Conclusion: AeroCast通过Transformer+MDN架构有效解决了非合作空中障碍物的多模态轨迹预测问题，在精度和实时性上均达到实用水平，适合机载部署。

Abstract: Autonomous aerial vehicles operating in shared airspace must predict the future positions of non-cooperative obstacles to plan evasive maneuvers before a collision becomes unavoidable. Unlike cooperative systems that share intent, non-cooperative obstacles such as birds, uncontrolled drones, or debris exhibit multi-modal motion that deterministic predictors cannot adequately represent. Existing methods either rely on recurrent encoders that propagate temporal information sequentially, limiting their ability to capture long-range kinematic precursors of maneuver initiation, or produce point forecasts that provide no distributional information to downstream planners. This paper presents AeroCast, a probabilistic trajectory prediction framework that combines a Transformer encoder with a Mixture Density Network output head to predict per-timestep Gaussian mixture distributions over future three-dimensional displacements. A translation-invariant consecutive displacement encoding and a calibration-oriented training objective address the input design and mode-degeneracy challenges specific to mixture-based aerial trajectory prediction. On a hybrid real-and-synthetic quadrotor corpus spanning nine motion categories, AeroCast reduces Average Displacement Error and Final Displacement Error by approximately 50% relative to the baselines over a five-second horizon, and achieves the lowest negative log-likelihood and Continuous Ranked Probability Score among all compared methods. Ablation analysis identifies velocity input and model capacity as the primary contributors to prediction quality, and positional encoding as essential for long-horizon trajectory coherence. AeroCast inference completes in 0.1ms per sample, compatible with real-time onboard deployment at 100Hz.

</details>


### [15] [RGB: RL Guided Whole-Body MPPI for Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25123)
*Yunsoo Seo,Sol Choi,Euncheol Im,Myo Taeg Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种RL引导的全身模型预测路径积分（MPPI）框架，将预训练的RL策略作为采样先验，通过MPPI在线校正来满足任务目标，无需重新训练策略。在29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人仿真中实现了280Hz的稳定高频控制，在直线行走和全身参考跟踪任务上优于纯RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习虽能实现鲁棒稳定性，但其行为与训练目标和命令接口紧密耦合，难以在不重新训练的情况下添加新的反馈目标。

Method: 提出RL引导的全身MPPI框架：将预训练RL策略作为采样先验来偏置MPPI rollout朝向动态可行行为；通过模块化MPPI成本项指定任务目标；MPPI通过持续在线校正RL先验来闭环满足这些目标，无需重新训练策略。

Result: 在29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人的MuJoCo仿真中实现平均280Hz的高频稳定控制；在相同命令接口下，任务级精度优于纯RL基线；能够校正直线行走中的系统性漂移，并跟踪通过成本项施加的额外全身参考信号。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效结合了RL的鲁棒稳定性与MPPI的灵活任务适应性，在不重新训练策略的前提下实现了更高的任务精度和额外的全身参考跟踪能力。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require whole-body controllers that are both robust and precise in contact-rich environments. While deep reinforcement learning (RL) achieves robust stability, its behavior is tightly coupled to the training objective and command interface, making it difficult to add new feedback objectives without retraining. In this study, we propose an RL guided whole-body model predictive path integral (MPPI) framework that acts as an add-on feedback controller on top of a pretrained RL policy. Instead of using RL policy as the final controller, we use it as a sampling prior that biases MPPI rollouts toward dynamically feasible behaviors. Task objectives are specified through modular MPPI cost terms, and MPPI closes the loop by continuously correcting the RL prior online to satisfy these objectives without retraining the policy. Simulations on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 humanoid in MuJoCo demonstrate stable high-rate control (average 280~Hz). The proposed method improves task-level precision over a pure RL baseline under the same command interface. This is achieved by correcting systematic drift during straight walking and tracking additional whole-body reference signals imposed through the cost.

</details>


### [16] [Causality-Based Parametric Control Barrier Function for Safe Multi-Vehicle Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25134)
*Yiwei Lyu,Caleb Chang,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于因果推理的参数化控制屏障函数（Causality-based Parametric-CBF），用于多车交互场景中的自适应安全控制，通过显式推理车辆间因果影响，在保证安全的同时提升任务效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全控制方法在多车交互中存在两个主要问题：1) 难以确定多车交互中周围车辆行为的因果关系；2) 被最坏情况分析主导，导致控制行为过于保守。亟需一种能有效推理车辆间因果影响并避免保守性的方法。

Method: 将Parametric-CBF扩展到多机器人交互场景，嵌入因果推理机制以显式建模车辆间因果影响，从而学习Causality-based Parametric-CBF，并基于此设计自适应安全控制器。

Result: 在多种高交互强度场景下，所提方法利用多车系统的运动灵活性，显著提升了任务效率。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模车辆间的因果影响，自适应安全控制器能够在保证安全的同时避免保守行为，提高交互密集场景下的任务效率。

Abstract: Safe control has been widely studied in various safety-critical applications, for instance, autonomous driving. In order to ensure the autonomous vehicle does not collide with other vehicles, it is essential to obtain an accurate expectation of surrounding vehicles' behavior and react adaptively. Instead of assuming fully cooperative and homogeneous vehicles using the same safety-critical controllers, recent works have been exploring different data-driven approaches to model the neighboring vehicles' underlying controllers with observed data. However, existing works either suffer from 1) the inter-vehicle influence during the multi-vehicle interaction, which makes it hard to determine the causality of surrounding vehicles' behavior in controller modeling, or 2) being dominated by the worst-case analysis, which may lead to overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we extend the prior work on Parametric-Control Barrier Function (Parametric-CBF) to multi-robot interactions with embedded causality inference to explicitly reason over the inter-vehicle influence. Given the learned Causality-based Parametric-CBF, we present an adaptive safety-critical controller that allows the ego vehicle to safely react to surrounding vehicles with the learned expectation. We demonstrate that by leveraging the motion flexibility among multi-vehicle systems, task efficiency can be greatly improved in various interaction-intensive scenarios.

</details>


### [17] [Memory Retrieval in Visuomotor Policies for Long-Horizon Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25136)
*Rutav Shah,Yisu Li,Femi Bello,Yuke Zhu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALO是一种基于注意力记忆检索机制的视觉运动策略，通过蒸馏VLM先验和稀疏注意力技术，解决了模仿学习中记忆检索的虚假相关性和累积误差问题，支持长达8分钟的历史信息检索用于长时程控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人在部分可观测环境（如家庭）中需要记忆来支持自主性，但现有Transformer注意力机制用于记忆检索时存在两个问题：(1) 策略可能学习到过去信息与预测动作之间的虚假相关性；(2) 闭环控制中预测误差累积导致模型漂移和级联失败。

Method: HALO包含两个核心组件：(1) 将视觉-语言模型(VLM)先验蒸馏到策略中，通过从演示轨迹生成记忆相关的问答对并联合训练视频问答目标，引导检索朝向任务相关信息，抑制虚假相关性；(2) 使用稀疏注意力机制，限制检索仅关注历史中最相关的部分，减少闭环控制中累积误差的影响。

Result: HALO能够从长达8分钟的过去经验中可靠检索任务相关信息，实现更可靠的长时程控制。

Conclusion: 通过蒸馏VLM先验和稀疏注意力，HALO有效解决了基于注意力记忆检索的视觉运动策略在长时程控制中的两个关键挑战，为通用机器人在部分可观测环境中的自主操作提供了可行的记忆检索方案。

Abstract: General-purpose robots operating in partially observable environments, such as homes, require memory to support autonomy. They must recall diverse information from the past, such as where objects were placed, which tasks a human partner has completed, and when an appliance was turned on. Achieving this versatility requires a general memory retrieval mechanism. Transformer architectures that use attention over long contexts for memory retrieval provide a promising approach, as they learn retrieval from data rather than relying on task-specific or hand-designed rules. However, directly incorporating them into imitation learning from offline data introduces two key challenges: (1) the policy may learn spurious correlations between past information and predicted actions, and (2) errors accumulate in memory due to prediction inaccuracies and their compounding interactions with the environment, causing model drift and cascading failures. To address both challenges, we introduce HALO, a visuomotor policy with an attention-based memory retrieval mechanism for long-horizon control. First, to suppress spurious correlations, HALO distills vision-language model (VLM) priors into the policy. It generates memory-dependent question--answer pairs from demonstration trajectories and trains jointly with a video question--answering objective, steering retrieval toward task-relevant information. Second, to reduce the impact of accumulated errors in memory during closed-loop control, HALO uses sparse attention that restricts retrieval to only the most relevant parts of the history. Together, these components enable more reliable long-horizon control by guiding the policy to retrieve task-relevant information from up to eight minutes of past experience. Project website: https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/HALO

</details>


### [18] [SwarmFly: A simulation platform for UAV swarm experiment design and validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25146)
*Abhishek Phadke,Karthik Kumar Vasudeva,Abhishek Joshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SwarmFly，一个基于MATLAB的模块化无人机集群仿真与测试平台，具备实时操作地图、四种集群协调模式、模拟IMU遥测和IP定位功能，并通过插件架构支持扩展。通过8个实验验证了各子系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机集群仿真工具存在维护不足、学习曲线陡峭、场景单一等问题，亟需一个全面、模块化的仿真平台来填补这一研究空白。

Method: SwarmFly采用MATLAB实现，包含实时操作地图、四种集群协调模式（领导者-跟随者、去中心化、异构中继、异构速度）、模拟IMU遥测和IP定位；采用插件架构允许研究人员在不修改核心代码的情况下添加行为、故障模型和分析工具，内置8个捆绑插件。

Result: 通过8个实验验证了集群的编队精度、抗风能力、故障恢复、能量续航和空域合规性等性能指标，各子系统均得到表征与验证。

Conclusion: SwarmFly平台完全运行于MATLAB环境中，其模块化设计支持向硬件在环测试、更大规模集群和更高保真度动力学的扩展，已开源发布。

Abstract: The initial development phase of UAV swarms largely depends on simulation for experimental design and validation, yet existing open-source tools are often unmaintained, have steep learning curves, or are built around a single fixed scenario. The need for a comprehensive, modular simulation platform is a recognized research gap. This paper presents SwarmFly, a MATLAB-based simulation and test platform for multi- UAV swarms that addresses these gaps. SwarmFly combines a real-time operational map, four swarm coordination modes (leader-follower, decentralized, heterogeneous relay, and heterogeneous speed), simulated IMU telemetry, and IP-based geolocation with a plugin architecture that lets researchers add behaviors, fault models, and analysis tools without touching the core code. Eight bundled plugins extend the base simulator into a full test harness. The SwarmFly platform exposes multi-agent aerial swarms to a wide range of internal and external disruptions, enabling observation and quantification of underlying swarm control and behavioral mechanisms. This study verifies and characterizes each subsystem through eight experiments that measure formation accuracy, wind tolerance, fault recovery, energy endurance, and airspace compliance. The platform runs entirely in MATLAB. Its modular design supports straightforward extension toward hardware-in-the-loop testing, larger swarms, and higher-fidelity dynamics. An open-source release is available at [https://github.com/abhishekphadke/SwarmFly.git]

</details>


### [19] [Toward Low-Latency Vision-Language Models with Doubly-Correct Predictions in Egocentric Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25160)
*Qitong Wang,Fan Du,Pranav Maneriker,Jihui Jin,Christopher Rasmussen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型（VLM）在具身智能与人机协作场景中的剪枝问题，提出了一种基于证据理性（rationale-informed）的剪枝策略，在保持模型效率的同时，确保预测既准确又具有可解释的证据支持（即“双重正确预测”），实验表明该方法在预测准确率和双重正确性上均优于现有剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM剪枝方法虽能减小模型尺寸和计算量以满足低延迟推理需求，但在人机协作场景中，剪枝后的模型往往无法同时保证预测准确性和证据可解释性（即双重正确预测），这会影响人机交互的安全性和用户信任。

Method: 提出一种理性信息引导的剪枝策略（rationale-informed pruning），在剪枝过程中对齐模型决策与证据定位，从而同时保持预测准确性和可解释性。

Result: 在自我中心视频数据集上的基准测试表明，该方法在预测准确率和双重正确预测指标上均优于现有剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 本文呼吁在追求VLM高效性的同时，应兼顾透明度、可审计性和安全性，推动负责任的人机交互与具身智能发展。

Abstract: The rapid rise of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in egocentric visual understanding has made low-latency inference in human-robot collaborative (HRC) tasks increasingly critical. Weight pruning techniques developed for VLMs to shrink model size and computation can be readily applied to satisfy the efficiency demands of on-board processing and real-time interactive robotics. Moreover, safe human-robot interaction demands pruning strategies that preserve doubly-correct predictions; outputs must be both accurate and evidentially grounded to mitigate risks and ensure user trust. In this paper, we present a new study of VLM pruning through the lens of doubly-correct prediction. Our experiments surprisingly show that existing pruning methods often preserve the right evidence localization but undermine correct prediction. To address this, we propose a rationale-informed pruning strategy that better aligns evidence with decisions. Benchmark results on egocentric video datasets demonstrate that our method not only achieves the highest prediction accuracy but also outperforms existing approaches in attaining doubly-correct predictions. We aim to stimulate research on efficient and reliable VLMs, ensuring accuracy-driven advances align with the transparency, auditability, and safety required for responsible human-robot interaction and embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [20] [fARfetch: Enabling Collocated AR-HRC in Large Visually Diverse Environments with VLM-Driven AR Content Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25162)
*Christian Fronk,Hanting Ye,David Hunt,Miroslav Pajic,Maria Gorlatova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: fARfetch是一个AR-HRC系统，结合共享语义环境映射、世界微缩表示和VLM驱动的AR视图管理，在大型户外环境中实现高效的人机协作。在30.5m的户外巡检任务中，相比非AR基线，完成时间快66%，脑力负荷降低43%，时间压力降低34%，挫败感降低66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强现实可以改善共位人机协作，但在大型、视觉多样的户外环境中，远距离和超越视觉视线的交互与内容可读性面临挑战。

Method: 提出fARfetch系统，包含三个组件：(i) AR头显与机器人共享语义环境映射，可视化检测到的地标以支持地标锚定的go-to指令；(ii) 上下文感知的共享环境世界微缩表示，用于精细路径规划；(iii) 视觉语言模型驱动的AR视图管理，联合调整虚拟内容的颜色、大小和方向以保持可读性。使用Meta Quest 3和Unitree Go2四足机器人实现，并在30.5m真实户外巡检任务中进行受试者内用户研究（N=13）。

Result: fARfetch相比非AR基线完成时间快66%；脑力负荷降低43%，时间需求降低34%，挫败感降低66%；自定义可读性调查表明系统有效维持了户外环境中虚拟内容的可读性。

Conclusion: fARfetch系统通过语义映射、微缩世界和VLM视图管理，有效解决了大型户外环境中AR-HRC的可读性和交互问题，显著提升了任务效率和用户体验。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) can improve collocated human-robot collaboration by making robot state and intent visible and enabling intuitive control, yet large, visually diverse environments like the outdoors challenge both interaction and content legibility, especially at long distances and beyond visual line of sight. We present fARfetch, an AR-HRC system that integrates (i) shared semantic environment mapping across an AR headset and robot that visualizes detected landmarks in AR to support landmark-grounded go-to commands, (ii) a context-aware world-in-miniature representation of the shared environment for fine-grained path authoring, and (iii) vision-language-model driven AR view management that jointly adapts virtual content color, size, and orientation to maintain legibility in large visually diverse environments. We implement fARfetch with a Meta Quest 3 headset and Unitree Go2 quadruped robot, and conduct a within-subjects user study (N=13) on a real-world large-scale (30.5m) outdoor inspection task. fARfetch yielded significantly faster completion times than a non-AR baseline (66%) and significantly lower workload in mental demand (-43%), temporal demand (-34%), and frustration (-66%). A custom legibility survey indicated fARfetch effectively maintained virtual content legibility in the large outdoor environment.

</details>


### [21] [Learning Perceptive Platform Adaptive Locomotion Controllers for Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25179)
*David Rytz,Kim Tien Ly,Ioannis Havoutis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在形态感知强化学习架构中如何整合感知信息以实现可部署的四足机器人控制，提出基于MorAL的批评家感知变体（MorAL+），在多个参考四足形态上训练通用控制器。实验表明，批评家仅感知策略在鲁棒性和跟踪一致性上优于盲策略，且比全感知策略对噪声更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足 locomotion 控制器存在两个主要局限：1）基于单机器人训练或盲策略的控制器缺乏跨形态泛化能力；2）全感知策略虽能感知地形但对噪声敏感，而盲控制器缺乏地形感知。因此，如何将感知信息有效地整合到形态感知的强化学习架构中成为一个关键问题。

Method: 基于MorAL框架，在多个参考四足机器人上使用自适应地形课程训练形态特化的通用控制器。比较了三种变体：盲基线策略、批评家仅感知变体（MorAL+）和全感知演员-批评家策略（PPAL）。策略在平坦和粗糙地形的仿真环境中评估，并在ANYmal硬件上部署。

Result: 批评家仅感知（MorAL+）在鲁棒性和跟踪一致性上优于盲基线策略，同时在感知噪声下比全感知策略（PPAL）更稳定。

Conclusion: 感知信息的放置位置（批评家端而非演员端）和课程设计是实现可扩展、形态感知 locomotion 的关键因素。

Abstract: Universal quadrupedal locomotion remains limited by the difficulty of integrating perception across diverse robot morphologies. State-of-the-art controllers rely on single-robot training or blind policies that omit real-time perception, leading to poor cross-embodiment generalization. Designing locomotion policies that remain robust across related quadruped morphologies while incorporating perception is challenging. Moreover, fully perceptive policies are often sensitive to noise, whereas blind controllers lack terrain awareness. In this work, we study how perception should be integrated into morphology-aware reinforcement learning architectures for deployable quadrupedal control. Building on MorAL, we train morphology-specialized universal controllers on multiple reference quadrupeds using adaptive terrain curricula. We compare a blind baseline, a critic-perceptive variant (MorAL+), and a fully perceptive actor-critic (PPAL). Policies are evaluated in simulation on flat and rough terrains, and deployed on ANYmal hardware. Results show that critic-only perception improves robustness and tracking consistency over blind baselines while remaining more stable than fully perceptive policies under perception noise. These findings highlight that perception placement and curriculum design are key factors for scalable, morphology-aware locomotion.

</details>


### [22] [RAVEN: Long-Horizon Reasoning & Navigation with a Visuo-Spatio-Temporal Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25206)
*Yixun Hu,Zhicheng Zheng,Lihan Zha,Chunwei Xing,Rajdeep Singh,Omar Hossain,Antonio Loquercio,Dhruv Shah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAVEN是一个基于视觉嵌入的智能记忆系统，通过向量数据库存储视觉特征（含位姿和时间信息），结合空间地图实现高效检索，用于长期机器人问答和导航任务，在多个基准上超越基于字幕的记忆系统，并以10倍更低成本达到前沿VLM水平，在真实机器人上成功部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期机器人部署需要紧凑且可扩展的记忆系统，能够保存细粒度视觉语义、在时空上锚定观察结果，并实现高效的存储与检索。现有基于图像转字幕的方法存在信息损失问题。

Method: RAVEN将视觉嵌入与位姿和时间信息存储在向量数据库中，并在空间地图上进行检索，直接操作视觉嵌入而非图像字幕，实现语义、空间和时间维度的精确检索。

Result: 在多个模拟和真实世界视频问答基准上，RAVEN一致超越基于字幕的记忆系统，以10倍更低的检索成本匹配前沿VLM在长时任务上的表现，并在Unitree Go1机器人上成功实现大规模室内环境的长时导航。

Conclusion: RAVEN通过直接存储和检索视觉嵌入，避免了字幕化过程中的信息损失，为长期机器人部署提供了一种高效、可扩展的记忆方案。

Abstract: Long-term robot deployment requires a compact and scalable memory that preserves fine-grained visual semantics, grounds observations in space and time, and enables efficient storage and retrieval. In this paper, we propose RAVEN, an agentic memory system for long-horizon robotic question answering and navigation. RAVEN stores visual embeddings with pose and time in a vector database, and grounds retrieval in a spatial map to answer queries and navigate to goals. By operating directly on visual embeddings, RAVEN avoids lossy image-to-text captioning and enables accurate semantic, spatial, and temporal retrieval at scale. Across several simulated and real-world video question-answering benchmarks, RAVEN consistently surpasses caption-based memory systems and matches frontier VLMs on long-horizon tasks at 10$\times$ lower retrieval cost. Finally, we instantiate RAVEN on a Unitree Go1 robot for the task of long-horizon navigation for natural language goal-reaching, and show successful deployment over several large indoor environments.

</details>


### [23] [Wear-Clearance-Impact Coupling in the Jansen Linkage: A Gait-Durability-Optimized Design Slows Joint Loosening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25208)
*Jichao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了Theo Jansen行走机构的间隙耦合正动力学模型，发现理想铰链模型低估峰值载荷约2倍，磨损集中在约10度载荷弧上且非均匀性导致局部间隙增长被低估约36倍，但优化设计在统计意义上仍具有耐久性优势（磨损低9-7倍，p<0.01）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究将关节视为理想无间隙铰链，磨损结果仅为相对排名，无法预测实际服役退化。本文旨在放松该理想化假设，建立包含间隙、接触、摩擦和磨损反馈的耦合动力学模型，评估真实工况下优化设计的耐久性。

Method: 建立Jansen腿正动力学模型，将旋转关节替换为间隙关节，采用Lankarani-Flores滞迟阻尼法向接触律和Ambrosio摩擦力模型，集成约束稳定化微分代数系统求解，并耦合Archard磨损定律形成磨损→间隙→冲击的反馈回路。采用16个随机相位系综进行统计分析。

Result: (1) 间隙模型峰值接触力~104 N vs 理想关节~48 N（~2倍放大），双间隙时升至~426 N；(2) 单轨迹非单调且可能反转设计排名，但系综统计显示优化关节磨损低9-7倍（p<0.01），双间隙时仍低~1.7倍；(3) 磨损集中在~10度载荷弧，假设均匀磨损会低估局部间隙增长~36倍。

Conclusion: 无间隙假设下的耐久性优势在混沌、多关节、非均匀磨损的耦合系综均值中仍然保持；首次提供了Jansen腿的间隙耦合正动力学模型，并给出了可证伪的实验检测协议。

Abstract: A companion study introduced joint durability into the dimensional design of the Theo Jansen walking linkage and found its classical "holy numbers" Pareto-dominated, but it modelled the revolute joints as ideal, clearance-free pins, so its wear figures were relative rankings, not a prediction of in-service degradation. Here we relax that idealization. We build a forward-dynamic model of the Jansen leg in which a revolute joint becomes a clearance joint with a continuous normal contact law (Lankarani-Flores, hysteresis-damped) and Ambrosio friction, integrated as a constraint-stabilized differential-algebraic system, and couple it to the Archard law in a wear->clearance->impact feedback loop. Three findings emerge. First, neglecting clearance underestimates the peak joint load: the clearance model gives a peak contact force of ~104 N at the load-bearing pin against ~48 N for the ideal joint (~2x amplification), rising to ~426 N when two joints carry clearance at once. Second, the coupling is strongly impact-sensitive--single trajectories are non-monotonic and can reverse the design ranking, a chaos consistent with the literature--so designs must be compared statistically; over an ensemble of 16 randomized phases the optimized joint is robustly more durable, with per-cycle wear ~9-7x lower (peak force ~4x lower) at one clearance joint and still ~1.7x lower on both with two (p<0.01 throughout). Third, the wear is strongly non-uniform--it concentrates on a ~10 deg load arc--so assuming uniform clearance growth underestimates local clearance growth by ~36x. The clearance-free durability advantage thus survives the chaotic, multi-joint, non-uniformly-worn coupling in the ensemble mean. We deliver the first clearance-coupled forward-dynamic model of the Jansen leg and specify a falsifiable protocol to test each prediction.

</details>


### [24] [RigPI: Dynamic Parameter Identification of Rigid Body via VLM-Seeded Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25212)
*Xincheng He,Rongrong Zhang,Wei Jiang,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RigPI是一个系统框架，利用视觉语言模型初始化、可微物理仿真和两阶段优化策略，从机器人-物体交互中识别刚体的惯性及摩擦参数，在真实实验中实现了高精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的物理参数（如惯性和摩擦）是高级机器人操作和数字孪生构建的基础，但从真实交互中获取这些参数面临传感噪声、建模误差和先验知识不足的挑战。

Method: 提出RigPI框架，集成视觉语义先验（VLM初始化）、力/力矩测量和运动观测，利用可微物理仿真器的梯度信息进行参数优化，并设计两阶段优化策略以抑制噪声并避免非物理解。

Result: 在包含旋转关节和棱柱关节的真实物体实验中，RigPI实现了准确且稳定的参数估计，并在真实机器人上成功复现操作轨迹，验证了参数感知的预测有效性。

Conclusion: RigPI框架在真实机器人系统识别任务中表现出高有效性和鲁棒性，能够生成物理一致的惯性和摩擦参数估计。

Abstract: Accurate physical parameter identification of manipulated objects is fundamental to advanced robotic manipulation and the construction of faithful digital twins. However, acquiring physically consistent inertial and frictional properties from real-world interactions remains challenging due to sensing noise, modeling errors, and limited prior knowledge. This paper presents \textbf{RigPI}, a systematic framework for identifying dynamic parameters of both unconstrained rigid bodies and multi-link rigid bodies during robot-object interaction. RigPI integrates vision-based semantic priors, force-torque measurements, and motion observations within a differentiable simulation pipeline. A vision-language model (VLM) provides informed initialization and a constrained search space, while gradient information from a differentiable physics simulator enables efficient and stable parameter refinement. The proposed two-stage optimization strategy alleviates sensitivity to noise and avoids physically implausible solutions. Extensive real-world experiments on objects with revolute and prismatic joints demonstrate that RigPI achieves accurate and stable parameter estimates, and successfully reproduces manipulation trajectories on a real robot with parameter-aware predictive validity. These results highlight the effectiveness and robustness of RigPI for real-world robotic system identification tasks.

</details>


### [25] [Swazure: Swarm Measurement of Pose for Flying Light Specks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25222)
*Hamed Alimohammadzadeh,Shahram Ghandeharizadeh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Swazure的协作方法，通过多架微型无人机（FLS）之间的协同合作，解决当FLS超出传感器最佳精度范围时无法追踪相对位姿的问题，并引入两种启发式算法处理大型FLS可能造成的遮挡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建3D多媒体显示系统时，FLS需要使用摄像头等传感器准确测量彼此间的相对位姿，但这些传感器仅在特定距离范围内提供最高精度。当点云数据要求FLS追踪超出其传感器最佳范围的另一个FLS时，现有技术面临挑战。

Method: 提出Swazure技术，通过FLS之间的协作来弥补缺失的传感器数据；实现物理数据独立性，使点云数据与传感器硬件解耦；针对大型FLS可能导致的遮挡问题，提出Move Obstructing和Move Source两种启发式算法。

Result: 中型FLS下，Swazure能100%定位FLS的邻居；大型FLS可能产生遮挡阻碍相对位姿量化；Move Obstructing启发式在最优情况下可解决约30%的遮挡问题。

Conclusion: Swazure能有效解决传感器最佳范围外的追踪问题，其中Move Obstructing启发式优于Move Source，但仍需进一步改进以应对更多遮挡场景。

Abstract: One may construct a 3D multimedia display using miniature drones configured with light sources, Flying Light Specks (FLSs). Swarms of FLSs localize to illuminate complex 3D shapes and animated sequences consistent with the coordinates of points in a point cloud. This requires FLSs to accurately measure their pose relative to one another using sensors such as cameras. Such sensors have a sweet range in which they provide the highest accuracy. A challenge is how an FLS tracks another FLS outside its sensor's sweet range, dictated by the point cloud data. We address this challenge by proposing a novel technique called Swazure that solves the missing sensor data using cooperation among FLSs. It implements physical data independence by abstracting the physical characteristics of the sensors, making point cloud data independent of the sensor hardware. The size of an FLS relative to the minimum distance between points of a point cloud is an important parameter. With medium-sized FLSs, Swazure is able to position 100% of the FLS's neighbors. Larger FLS sizes may result in potential obstructions that prevent Swazure from quantifying relative pose. We present two heuristics, Move Obstructing and Move Source, to address this limitation. Our experimental results show the superiority of the Move Obstructing heuristic which resolves approximately 30% of obstructions in the worst case scenario.

</details>


### [26] [Spatio-Temporal Retrieval-based Priors for Adaptive Computational Teaching in Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25224)
*Deepak Edakkattil Gopinath,Xiongyi Cui,Jonathan DeCastro,Avinash Balachandran,Guy Rosman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模仿学习的自适应教学计算模型，通过最近邻检索和交叉注意力机制，在低数据场景下对驾驶教学中的长期师生交互历史进行时序推理，并在半合成和真实数据集上验证了其优于非自适应基线和多种自适应模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的自动化教学系统在高性能驾驶等复杂运动任务中，仅依赖局部上下文推理，无法考虑学生学习的长期时序特性以及师生重复互动的累积影响，缺乏自适应能力。

Method: 提出一种基于模仿学习的自适应教学模型，包含一个专用的时序推理模块，利用最近邻检索和交叉注意力先验，从历史交互中筛选语义相似的少量交互进行推理，结合编码器-解码器的并发教学模型，在低数据场景下实现自适应教学。

Result: 在基于Waymo Open Motion Dataset的半合成纵向师生交互数据集和小规模真实自然主义模拟器赛车教学数据集上，所提出的自适应教学模型（含最近邻检索和交叉注意力先验）一致优于非自适应基线和多种采用不同先验与时序融合机制的自适应模型。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型能够有效利用历史交互信息进行时序推理，在低数据场景下实现自适应教学，为自动化教学系统在处理长期师生互动方面提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learning-based automated coaching systems for complex motor tasks such as high-performance driving remain limited in the ability to be adaptive by their reliance only on local, context-dependent reasoning, failing to account for the long-term temporal nature of student learning and the cumulative impact of repeated teacher-student interactions. In this paper, we propose an imitation learning based computational model for adaptive teaching with a dedicated temporal reasoning module that can reason over the interaction history under low-data regimes. To compensate for limited amounts of interactive training data, and based on the repetitive nature of the teaching process, the model relies on a nearest neighbor retrieval and cross attention prior, reasoning only on a narrowed-down set of semantically similar past interactions with an encoder-decoder based concurrent teaching model. We validate our approach with (i) a novel semi-synthetic closed-loop longitudinal student-teacher interaction dataset based on Waymo Open Motion Dataset and (ii) a small-scale real-world naturalistic simulator race coaching dataset. Our results reveal the consistent advantage of our adaptive teaching model with the nearest neighbor retrieval and cross-attention prior over a non-adaptive baseline as well as a suite of adaptive models that differ in their choice of priors and temporal fusion mechanisms.

</details>


### [27] [GRAFT: Graph-Based Affordance Transfer via Part Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25241)
*Mengying Lin,Utkarsh Mishra,Ajay Mandlekar,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRAFT是一个基于几何感知对应关系的零样本操作迁移框架，仅需每个物体一次演示，通过部件级和顶点级描述符实现从已见过物体到未见物体的操作技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于学习的机器人操作方法需要大量演示数据，在少样本场景下表现不佳；而基于语义检索的迁移方法忽略了对于操作至关重要的几何相似性。因此需要一种能够利用几何信息实现少量演示下的操作迁移方法。

Method: 将物体表示为基于部件的图结构，包含部件级描述符（用于全局实例检索和部件对应）和顶点级描述符（用于细粒度接触点匹配）。对于未见物体，首先从演示缓冲池中检索功能与几何最相似的实例，对齐功能部件，然后通过逐点对应传播接触点。

Result: 零样本操作迁移，仅需每个物体一次演示即可完成。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过结合几何和语义信息，在仅需一次演示的条件下实现了有效的零样本操作迁移，优于仅依赖语义的方法。

Abstract: Generalizing robotic manipulation to unseen objects remains challenging, as learning-based approaches require many demonstrations and fail in few-shot settings. Prior work transfers affordances through semantic retrieval, but semantics alone neglect geometric similarity, which is critical for manipulation. We propose GRAFT, a geometry-aware correspondence framework for zero-shot manipulation transfer using only one demonstration per object. Objects are represented as part-based graphs, where part-level descriptors support global instance retrieval and part correspondence, and vertex-level descriptors enable fine-grained contact point matching. For an unseen object, our method first retrieves the most functionally and geometrically similar instance from the demonstration buffer with aligned functional parts, and finally propagates the contact points through point-wise correspondence.

</details>


### [28] [An Integrated Hardware-Software Design for Low-Data Spatial Defect Detection in Robotic Visual Inspection with Hybrid Optoelectronic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25277)
*Chaoqing Tang,Jiaxuan Li,Huanze Zhuang,Guiyun Tian,Chao Wang,Yihao Ouyang,Wenzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种软硬件集成光电架构，通过DMD光学卷积层和块压缩感知实现非成像低数据范式，结合CLIP语言引导实现缺陷形状级检测，在降低90%数据量和60%计算量的同时保持等效精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人视觉检测中数据过载和耗时的形状级标注问题，减少对人工标注的依赖。

Method: 1) 传感器在环策略：将DMD重构为物理光学卷积层，实现光子域特征提取；2) 基于块的压缩感知：将空间信息编码为低维时序信号；3) CLIP语言引导：利用自然语言描述引导网络注意力图对准缺陷形状；4) 提出LAA指标量化形状级定位性能。

Result: 相比传统成像，对Vision Transformer数据量减少90%，对CNN计算量减少60%，同时保持等效精度。

Conclusion: 该低数据范式为工业自动化中大数据流、高采集成本或边缘资源受限场景提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: To address data overload and inefficient shape-level annotation in robotic visual inspection, this paper proposes a hardware-software integrated optoelectronic architecture. A non-imaging, low-data paradigm is established to minimize annotation dependency. First, a sensor-in-the-loop strategy reconfigures a Digital Micromirror Device (DMD) as a physical optical convolutional layer, enabling photonic-domain feature extraction that unifies sensing hardware and processing software. To suppress data volume at the source, a block-based compressed sensing strategy encodes spatial information into low-dimensional temporal signals, drastically reducing redundancy. Subsequently, to bypass laborious manual defect shape annotation, natural language descriptions guide the network to align with highly generalizable features from Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP), steering the attention maps of the optoelectronic neural network toward defect shapes. Furthermore, a Localization Accuracy for Attention (LAA) metric is proposed to quantify shape-level defect localization performance. Experiments on transparent material defect detection validate the system's effectiveness. Parametric analysis reveals how measurement matrices, compression ratios, and block sizes affect accuracy. Results show that, compared to traditional imaging, the proposed architecture maintains equivalent accuracy while reducing data volume by 90% for Vision Transformers and computational workload by 60% for Convolutional Neural Networks. This low-data paradigm offers an efficient solution for industrial automation scenarios involving massive data streams, high acquisition costs, or constrained edge resources.

</details>


### [29] [TurboMPC: Fast, Scalable, and Differentiable Model Predictive Control on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24039)
*Gabriel Bravo-Palacios,Jianghan Zhang,Zachary Pestrikov,Brian Plancher,Thomas Lew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TurboMPC是一个完全运行在GPU上的可微分MPC求解器，支持不等式约束、隐式积分器、跨时间耦合成本等复杂功能。它结合SQP、ADMM内层求解器、隐式微分和JAX-CUDA实现，在仿真中相比CPU和GPU求解器分别实现最高15倍和58倍加速，并在真实赛车上验证了基于贝叶斯优化的参数调优效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人领域越来越多地依赖GPU进行并行仿真、大规模学习和神经网络推理，现有MPC求解器无法高效运行在GPU上，同时难以兼顾快速性、可微性和机器人所需的表达能力（如不等式约束、隐式积分器等）。

Method: TurboMPC结合了序列二次规划（SQP）、交替方向乘子法（ADMM）内层求解器、隐式微分技术，并采用JAX-CUDA协同设计实现GPU上的高效计算与易用性。

Result: 在仿真中，TurboMPC在约束规划、人形机器人模仿学习和带神经网络成本函数的强化学习任务上，相比最先进的CPU和GPU可微分求解器分别实现最高15倍和58倍加速。在真实赛车上，通过贝叶斯优化进行GPU批量参数调优，驾驶速度显著优于手动调优基线；且可扩展至超过8000个节点的规划时域。

Conclusion: TurboMPC证明了完全基于GPU的可微分MPC求解器在机器人控制中的可行性和优越性，能够显著加速仿真和实际部署中的MPC任务，并支持大规模规划时域和自动参数调优。

Abstract: Robotics increasingly relies on GPUs for parallel simulation, large-scale learning, and neural-network inference. For model predictive control (MPC) to scale with this paradigm, solvers must run efficiently on this hardware while remaining fast, differentiable, and compatible with expressive MPC formulations used in robotics. We present TurboMPC, a differentiable MPC solver that runs entirely on the GPU and supports state and control inequality constraints, implicit integrators, cross-time-coupled costs, and slack variables. TurboMPC combines sequential quadratic programming (SQP), an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) inner solver, implicit differentiation, and a co-designed JAX-CUDA implementation for efficiency and ease of use. In simulation, we validate TurboMPC on constrained planning, humanoid imitation learning, and reinforcement learning with neural-network cost function tasks, achieving up to $15\times$ and $58\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art CPU and GPU differentiable solvers, respectively. We deploy TurboMPC on a full-scale car for minimum-time racing and find that batched, GPU-accelerated tuning of MPC parameters via Bayesian optimization yields significantly faster driving than a hand-tuned baseline. TurboMPC also scales to planning horizons of over $8000$ knot points while maintaining control of the vehicle. We open-source TurboMPC at: https://github.com/ToyotaResearchInstitute/turbompc

</details>


### [30] [DynaMOMA: Instantaneous Prediction of Grasp Poses for Mobile Manipulation of Dynamic Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25295)
*Zhinan Yu,Junyan Xu,Jiazhao Zhang,Zheng Qin,Yijie Tang,Yuhang Huang,Yihan Cao,Zhiyuan Yu,Yongjun Wang,Renjiao Yi,Chenyang Zhu,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种动态移动操作框架，结合基于锚点的扩散模型预测短期抓取轨迹，并通过全身强化学习策略控制移动机械臂，在仿真和真实实验中实现了对动态物体的有效抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作中动态物体的操作极具挑战性，机器人需要协调移动基座和机械臂以适配持续变化的目标姿态，现有方法难以从动态观测中预测时间一致的短期抓取轨迹。

Method: 提出动态移动操作框架，包含：1) 基于锚点的扩散模型，生成时间一致的短期抓取轨迹；2) 全身强化学习策略，将预测轨迹编码为紧凑特征作为输入；3) 预期引导奖励，通过自适应地将目标从当前抓取观测转移到预测轨迹，赋予策略预期抓取能力。

Result: 在Isaac Gym仿真中，该方法在多种动态物体移动操作场景和抓取指标上均表现优异；在真实实验中，预测器和策略展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了动态物体移动操作中轨迹预测与全身控制的耦合问题，在仿真和真实场景中均取得良好效果。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a fundamental robotics task and has advanced rapidly in recent years, enabling robots to navigate, reach, and interact with objects in complex environments. However, mobile manipulation of dynamic objects remains highly challenging, as robots must coordinate the mobile base and arm while adapting to continuously evolving target poses. A key challenge lies in predicting temporally consistent short-horizon grasp trajectories from dynamic observations. In this work, we propose \ours{}, a dynamic mobile manipulation framework that couples instantaneous grasp trajectory prediction with whole-body control policy. Our predictor uses an anchor-based diffusion model to generate temporally consistent short-horizon grasp trajectories conditioned on historical observations. The predicted trajectories are then encoded as compact features and fed to a whole-body reinforcement learning policy, which controls the mobile manipulator for dynamic grasping. We further introduce a anticipation-guided reward that equips the policy with an anticipatory grasping horizon by adaptively shifting the target from the current grasp observation to the instantaneously predicted grasp trajectory. Through extensive experiments in Isaac Gym simulation, we show that our method achieves strong performance in mobile manipulation of dynamic objects across diverse settings and grasping metrics. Furthermore, our predictor and policy demonstrate strong generalizability in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [31] [WaveForward: An Omnidirectional Passive Wheeled Quadruped Robot with Casters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25299)
*Chuanlin Zhao,Qifeng Zheng,Shuhan Wang,Tiancheng Ma,Weixian Lin,Xin Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本被动轮腿机器人，每腿安装标准万向轮，采用非对称Actor-Critic结构和基于速度指令的底座姿态调整策略，实现全方位移动，运输成本较纯腿式运动降低最高89.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动驱动轮腿机器人因集成轮组件需要专用硬件和电气设计，成本较高。本文旨在设计一种低成本被动轮腿机器人，利用标准万向轮实现全方位移动能力。

Method: 采用非对称Actor-Critic结构利用被动万向轮角度和速度的特权信息；基于速度指令的底座姿态调整策略，通过驱动关节改变万向轮底座关节轴姿态以调整推进方向；实现多种推进模式以不同程度地产生万向轮扭转振荡并转化为推进力。

Result: 通过障碍滑雪测试和模式切换实验，被动轮腿四足机器人实现全方位移动多样性，运输成本较纯腿式运动降低高达89.1%。

Conclusion: 被动轮腿机器人能以低成本实现全方位移动，并显著降低运输能耗，适用于长距离运输场景。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots possess both agile mobility for traversing complex terrains and high efficiency, making them suitable for long-distance transportation applications. Conventional actuated wheeled robots require specialized hardware and electrical design due to the incorporation of wheel components. We propose a novel and low-cost passive wheeled legged robot equipped with standard casters on each leg to obtain omnidirectional mobility. The control method employs an asymmetric actor-critic structure, enabling the utilization of the privileged information of the passive caster's angles and velocities. We develop a caster base posture adjustment strategy based on velocity commands, utilizing actuated joints to modify the caster base joint axis posture and thereby adjust the propulsion direction of the casters. Moreover, we implemented multiple propulsion modes to achieve varying degrees of caster twisting oscillation, converting these into propulsive force. We conducted a slalom test and mode switch experience, which shows the passive wheeled quadruped could achieve omnidirectional movement versatility, and reduce the cost of transport (COT) by up to 89.1% with respect to legged motion.

</details>


### [32] [AI Coaching for Accelerating Human Skill Development with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25337)
*Wei Wang,Enlin Gu,Antonio Loquercio,Haimin Hu,Rahul Mangharam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过强化学习框架训练AI教练，在无人机竞速任务中通过自适应共享控制和因果影响建模来加速人类运动技能发展，实验表明该方法显著优于现有AI教练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助系统（如副驾）过度协助会导致人类技能退化，缺乏能够战略性地搭建脚手架、在适当时机退后以促进"有效失败"学习的人工智能教练。本文旨在通过形式化建模和强化学习训练，使AI代理能够作为教练加速人类运动技能发展。

Method: 1) 将交互式AI教练过程形式化为非合作动态博弈，学习者优化任务表现，教练以学习者独立能力为目标；2) 构建强化学习框架，结合自适应共享控制与教练因果影响学习演化的概率模型；3) 在第一次人称视角无人机竞速任务上进行33人用户研究。

Result: 在33人用户研究中，所提出的方法在人类学习成果上显著优于最先进的AI教练基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过形式化的博弈建模和强化学习，AI教练能够通过战略性地提供/撤除辅助来促进人类运动技能发展，而非过度取代人类控制，从而避免技能萎缩问题。

Abstract: AI copilots can substantially boost human performance through shared control, but excessive assistance can induce over-reliance and skill atrophy. This paper studies how an embodied AI agent can act as a coach that accelerates human motor-skill development. We argue that effective coaching requires strategic scaffolding and stepping back that are aligned with the learner's capability, allowing productive failures that drive learning. We formalize the interactive AI coaching process as a non-cooperative dynamic game in which the learner optimizes task performance while the coach targets the learner's independent competence. Building on this formalism, we develop a reinforcement learning framework combining adaptive shared control with probabilistic models of the coach's causal influence on skill evolution, enabling tractable training of coaching policies. A comprehensive user study (N=33) on first-person-view drone racing shows significant gains in human learning outcomes over state-of-the-art AI coaching baselines.

</details>


### [33] [Self Capacitive Tactile Sensor System designed for Companion Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25348)
*Mohsin Ali,Hidenobu Sumioka,Shuhei Ikemoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自电容原理的简单、可扩展、硬件友好的触觉传感系统，用于伴侣型人形机器人。该系统采用单层导电织物和导电织物线架构，无需复杂电极图案。通过柔性印刷电路制造了100点传感器阵列，并验证了100 Hz和1000 Hz采样频率能可靠捕获各种交互类型。基于FPGA实现的决策树分类器实现了实时推理，满足HIRO-chan机器人的全身触觉传感需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统触觉传感系统存在多层结构复杂、布线繁琐、成本高、可扩展性差等问题，难以在保持低计算负载的同时实现全身触觉传感的实时低延迟检测。

Method: 基于自电容原理，使用单层导电织物和导电织物线架构；在柔性印刷电路上制造100点传感器阵列；在FPGA上实现决策树分类器，将实时推理从树莓派4卸载到FPGA。

Result: 10 Hz采样频率不足，会丢失瞬态事件；100 Hz和1000 Hz能可靠捕获并清晰区分轻触、慢敲、快敲和击打四种交互类型；FPGA推理延迟低且功耗开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 该设计完全满足HIRO-chan机器人的触觉传感需求，适用于HIRO-chan及其他伴侣机器人的全身触觉传感。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for humanoid robots to achieve safe physical interaction, dexterous manipulation, and truly human-like responsiveness. However, the design of such systems remains challenging. Conventional approaches often suffer from complex multilayer structures, intricate wiring, high cost, and poor scalability, making it difficult to realize full-body tactile sensing with real-time, low-latency detection while maintaining minimal computational load on the robot's main processor. In this work, we present a simple, scalable and hardware friendly tactile sensing system for a companion humanoid robot based on the self-capacitance principle. The proposed sensor system employs a single conductive fabric layer with a conductive fabric wire architecture and does not require intricate electrode patterning. Scalability was demonstrated by fabricating a 100-point sensor array on a flexible printed circuit (FPC). Evaluation across sampling frequencies showed that 10 Hz is insufficient and misses transient events, whereas 100 Hz and 1000 Hz reliably capture and clearly distinguish all interaction types: gentle touch, slow tapping, fast tapping, and hitting. A decision-tree classifier was implemented directly on the FPGA, offloading real-time inference from the Raspberry Pi 4 with minimal latency and negligible power overhead. This design fully meets the tactile sensing requirements of the HIRO-chan robot and is well-suited for full-body tactile sensing in HIRO-chan and other companion robots.

</details>


### [34] [Decoupling Semantics and Geometric Grounding: Spatial Visual Prompts for Language-Conditioned Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25360)
*Yanzhe Tang,Xinyu Shao,Yuxuan Hu,Siyu Chen,Bowen Yang,Yajun Gao,Tongtong Cao,Xiu Li,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SVP-IL提出了一种解耦的视觉-语言-动作架构，通过从动作生成循环中显式提取空间视觉定位，利用视觉语言基础模型将指令解析为零样本几何掩码作为空间视觉提示，并通过轻量级特征融合注入连续动作生成器，在数据受限的模仿学习场景下显著提升了机器人操作的精确性和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端VLA模型将语义推理和空间控制耦合，导致对齐瓶颈，在数据受限的模仿学习中难以实现精确的目标消歧。

Method: 提出SVP-IL解耦架构：(1) 利用视觉语言基础模型将指令解析为零样本几何掩码（SVP）；(2) 通过轻量级直接特征级融合机制将SVP注入连续动作生成器，提供显式且无干扰的空间梯度引导。

Result: 在50-100条演示的低数据条件下，高度模糊语言条件任务成功率从24.0%提升至39.5%；标准基准达67.8%；真实机器人实验验证了鲁棒性和数据效率。

Conclusion: 解耦语义推理与空间控制的范式能有效缓解VLA模型的对齐瓶颈，在数据受限场景下显著提升机器人操作性能和稳定性。

Abstract: While end-to-end Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show promise in robotic manipulation, their monolithic paradigm inherently couples semantic reasoning and spatial control. This creates a severe alignment bottleneck, limiting precise target disambiguation in data-constrained imitation learning. To overcome this, we propose SVP-IL, a decoupled architecture that explicitly extracts spatial visual grounding from the action generation loop. By leveraging vision-language foundation models, we parse instructions into zero-shot geometric masks, translating language into explicit Spatial Visual Prompts (SVP). These priors are injected into a continuous action generator via a lightweight direct feature-level fusion mechanism. This integration provides explicit and uncorrupted spatial gradient guidance while ensuring highly stable optimization under low-data regimes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SVP-IL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art VLAs and pure visuomotor baselines. Trained on as few as 50 to 100 demonstrations, SVP-IL improves average success rates on highly ambiguous language-conditioned tasks from 24.0% to 39.5%, achieving 67.8% on standard benchmarks. Real-world robotic experiments further validate its robustness and data efficiency in unstructured physical environments.

</details>


### [35] [Reliability-Asymmetric Spacecraft Autonomy: Co-Designing a Capable Learned GNC Stack with a Verified, Adaptation-Aware Runtime Shield](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25366)
*Alireza Shojaei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AMPLE-GNC，一个结合基础模型指挥官（映射自然语言到PDDL+）、约束筛选验证器、故障自适应控制器和运行时防护盾的三层制导导航控制系统，在6-DOF Basilisk测试平台上验证了其能力与可认证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深空任务需要既具备能力又可认证的星载自主性，但基于规则的自主性虽可认证却脆弱，学习型自主性能力强却难以验证，两者难以兼得。

Method: 三层架构：(1) 能力路径包含小型基础模型指挥官（微调360M预训练模型+文法约束解码）、约束筛选验证器、故障自适应控制器（快速运动自适应推断潜在执行器故障）；(2) 运行时防护盾包含9个线性时序逻辑不变量，由Kind 2模型检查器机器验证预测器合理性；(3) 采用分裂共形恢复截止证明书和自适应感知接合机制平衡安全与恢复。

Result: 指挥官84%规划器可执行动作，去泄露测试泛化38%精确匹配/51%动作匹配；故障自适应控制器恢复97.8%执行器符号故障和94.4%连续增益故障；防护盾保持控制器94.5%自主同时捕获非恢复情况。

Conclusion: AMPLE-GNC成功弥合了学习型自主性的能力与形式化验证的可认证性之间的鸿沟，通过分层架构在能力与安全性之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Deep-space missions need onboard autonomy that is both capable and certifiable. Rule-based autonomy is certifiable but brittle, while learned autonomy is capable but hard to verify. We present AMPLE-GNC, a three-tier guidance, navigation, and control stack. Its capability path combines a small foundation-model commander that maps natural language to PDDL+, a constraint-screening verifier, and a fault-adaptive controller. All three are bounded by a runtime shield with nine linear-temporal-logic invariants whose predictor soundness is machine-checked by the Kind 2 model checker. On a 6-DOF Basilisk testbed, we make three contributions. First, we deploy an edge commander. Fine-tuning a pretrained 360M model with grammar-constrained decoding gives a hard output-validity guarantee and 84% planner-executable actions. On a de-leaked test, novel-phrasing generalization is 38% exact and 51% action, rising to 48% exact after phrasing-diversity re-finetuning; we separate syntactic validity from semantic accuracy. Second, we introduce a fault-adaptive controller. Rapid Motor Adaptation infers latent actuator faults online and recovers 97.8% of actuator-sign faults and 94.4% of continuous-gain faults within the training randomization envelope. Fault-unaware PD and from-scratch end-to-end RL both score 0%, while the strongest classical-adaptive baseline reaches 55% on continuous gain. Beyond the envelope, a split-conformant retrain scores 57-67%, and adding 4x more in-regime data worsens performance, showing that randomization breadth, not data volume, drives generalization. Robustness is flat under star-tracker noise to 0.005. Third, we show that a latching safe-hold shield can suppress even a capable controller. A split-conformal recovery-deadline certificate with adaptation-aware engagement reconciles safety and recovery, keeping the controller 94.5% autonomous while still catching non-recovery.

</details>


### [36] [Commerge: Communication-Efficient, Robust, and Fast LiDAR Map Merging Framework for Multi-Robot Coordination in Resource-Constrained Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25386)
*Hogyun Kim,Jiwon Choi,Juwon Kim,Geonmo Yang,Seokhwan Jeong,Hyungtae Lim,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Commerge 提出了一种基于图论选择性数据交换的通信高效多机器人 LiDAR 地图合并框架，在保持对齐精度的同时将机器人间通信量减少高达 5000 倍（99.98%），例如在 HeLiPR 数据集上从 7,000MB 降至 1.3MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人 LiDAR 地图合并中，服务器与机器人之间传输海量传感器数据导致通信成为瓶颈，尤其在通信受限环境中问题尤为突出。

Method: 将地图合并建模为交换图上的三级级联优化问题：第一级选取顺序重叠子集，第二级实现平衡传输成本，第三级优化几何-感知最优性，从而仅选择少量关键扫描实现鲁棒地图合并。

Result: 在五个公开数据集和四个自建数据集（涵盖洞穴、行星模拟、室内和室外校园环境）上，实现高达 99.98% 的数据交换减少，对齐精度与全量传输方法相当，在嵌入式到桌面平台均表现稳定。

Conclusion: 仅需精心选择少量扫描子集即可实现鲁棒的地图合并，Commerge 在极端通信受限环境下具有显著的实际应用价值。

Abstract: By maintaining global consistency across robot teams, multi-robot LiDAR map merging enables faster exploration and efficient area coverage. However, map merging requires exchanging massive sensor data between the server and robots, making communication the bottleneck, especially in communication-constrained environments. Therefore, we present Commerge, a communication-efficient map merging framework that achieves bandwidth reduction through graph-theoretic selective data exchange. By doing so, our Commerge reduces inter-robot communication by up to 5,000x while maintaining alignment accuracy. Our key insight is that only a small subset of carefully selected scans is sufficient for robust map merging. We formulate this as a three-stage cascaded optimization problem on an exchange graph, where vertices represent robot keyframes and edges denote candidate inter-robot loops. Through three cascade stages, we select a sequentially overlapped, balanced-transmission-cost, and geometrically-perceptually optimal scan subset that preserves alignment quality while reducing communication. Unlike existing approaches that either transmit whole scans, which require GB-scale data exchange, or employ naive downsampling, our approach exchanges only MB-scale data while achieving comparable alignment accuracy. Extensive evaluation on five public datasets and four in-house datasets covering cave, planetary-analog, indoor, and outdoor campus environments shows up to 99.98% reduction in data exchange (e.g., from 7,000MB to 1.3MB on the HeLiPR dataset), while maintaining alignment performance across embedded to desktop platforms. The supplementary materials are available at https://sparolab.github.io/research/commerge.

</details>


### [37] [Large-Scale Tunnel Air--Ground Collaboration With FLISP: Fast LiDAR-IMU Synchronized Path Planne](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25393)
*Fenghe Guo,Runjie Shen,Chenyang Sun,Junrui Zhang,Quanxi Zhan,Yongchun Wang,Junjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FLISP框架，一种无需地图的协同UGV-UAV水电站隧道检测路径规划方法，通过单一LiDAR-IMU单元驱动双平台同步路径生成，在1.2公里隧道中实现100%成功率与~7ms延迟，较传统方法提速7倍至三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水电站隧道检测依赖人工方法，效率低且危险；现有基于地图的机器人方法存在栅格化开销大、采样不稳定等结构瓶颈，难以在特征退化的线性基础设施中可靠运行。

Method: (1) 统一架构：单UGV搭载LiDAR-IMU联合驱动双平台路径生成；(2) 平台专用求解器：增强萤火虫算法用于UGV避障，动态迭代优化器用于UAV飞行；(3) 分层优化策略：确保运动学可行性，避免状态估计漂移。全程无需构建全局地图。

Result: 在1.2公里运营隧道中，FLISP实现100%成功率，延迟约7ms；相较于基于栅格的Fast-LIO2+A*提速7倍，相较于基于采样的LIO-SAM+RRT*提升三个数量级。

Conclusion: FLISP为特征退化的线性基础设施（如水电站隧道）提供了一种可扩展、无地图的机器人协同检测方案，有效规避了传统地图方法的瓶颈。
