Conclusion: ForceBand作为一种低成本、可扩展的力增强演示采集方案，有效弥补了现有演示数据缺少力信息的不足，显著提升了机器人在力敏感操作任务上的表现。

Abstract: Human demonstrations are a scalable data source for learning robot manipulation policies. However, common sources of human demonstration data, such as motion-capture trajectories and internet videos, capture mostly motion and appearance while missing the contact forces that are critical for force-sensitive manipulation. In this paper, we introduce ForceBand, a low-cost wrist-worn sEMG system that turns human muscle activity into force-enriched demonstrations. We first collect a 10-hour multimodal dataset containing egocentric video, sEMG, IMU, and fingertip force measurements across diverse actions and objects. Using this dataset, we pre-train an EMG2Force model that predicts per-finger forces from sEMG and IMU signals. After a short user-specific calibration, users can collect target-task demonstrations using only ForceBand and video; EMG2Force then labels these demonstrations with per-finger force traces, producing force-augmented demonstrations for robot policy learning. Experiments show that ForceBand recovers fine-grained fingertip interactions with over 50% lower force prediction error than vision-based baselines and achieves an 87% success rate on pick, squeeze, and place tasks that require object-specific force control across objects with diverse shapes, sizes, and weights. Project website: https://forceband-emg.github.io

</details>


### [76] [Learning Action Priors for Cross-embodiment Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26095)
*Dong Jing,Tianqi Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Jinman Zhao,Zelong Sun,Li Erran Li,Zhiwu Lu,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两阶段训练框架，先通过流匹配预训练动作模块的运动先验，再与视觉-语言模型对齐，显著提升了VLA策略在跨具身任务中的收敛速度、成功率和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将动作模块几乎从零学习物理运动，缺乏显式运动先验，导致早期优化需同时发现时序动作动力学和跨模态对齐，跨具身场景下挑战更大。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage 1 使用流匹配预训练轻量级编码器-解码器动作模块，仅基于无条件动作轨迹学习时序运动结构；Stage 2 通过解码器复用和早期潜层蒸馏将运动先验迁移至VLA训练，同时支持端到端策略微调。

Result: 在13个跨具身任务（模拟和真实）上验证，相比无动作先验的VLA训练，模型收敛更快、成功率更高，数据稀缺的真实任务上表现更强。扩展Stage1动作数据可提升下游VLA性能。

Conclusion: 预训练运动先验能有效提升VLA策略的泛化能力和数据效率，动作数据规模与下游性能正相关。

Abstract: Most Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models build on a Vision-Language Model (VLM) backbone by attaching an action module and optimizing the full policy jointly. This design inherits strong visual and linguistic priors from the VLM, but leaves the action module to learn physical motion almost from scratch. As a result, the policy lacks an explicit motion prior, forcing early optimization to simultaneously discover temporal action dynamics and cross-modal alignment, a challenge further amplified in cross-embodiment settings. In this work, we propose to pretrain the action module with motion priors before cross-modal VLA alignment. Specifically, we introduce a two-stage training framework that equips the action module with cross-embodiment temporal motion structure before VLA training begins. In Stage~1, a lightweight flow-matching-based encoder-decoder action module efficiently learns temporal motion structure solely from unconditioned action trajectories, without processing visual or language tokens. In Stage~2, this learned prior is transferred to VLA training through decoder reuse and early-stage latent distillation, aligning visual-language features with the action embedding space while still allowing end-to-end policy refinement. In addition, the trained encoder serves as a compact history compressor, summarizing state-action histories into a single temporal context token for history-aware modeling at negligible cost. Extensive experiments across 13 diverse cross-embodiment tasks on both simulated and real-world platforms validate the effectiveness of our approach. Compared with VLA training without action priors, our model achieves faster convergence, higher success rates, and substantially stronger performance on data-scarce real-world tasks. Moreover, scaling up the action data in Stage~1 yields a more generalizable action prior that directly improves downstream VLA performance.

</details>

### [77] [Minimization of a One Class of Maximum Functions with Applications to Some Eigenvalue Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25106)
*İpek Cafer Ismayilov,Taner Büyükköroğlu,Vakif Dzhafarov*

Main category: math.OC

Abstract: Gradient methods are widely used for large-scale optimization, yet their practical convergence performance depends critically on step-size selection. However, a unified structural understanding of reliable spectral step sizes and a theoretically grounded mechanism for ordering step sequences remain underdeveloped. This paper addresses both gaps. We first establish a unified structural framework encompassing four families of spectral step sizes: the general Huang class, its rank-one subclass, the narrow Huang class, and the Gu-Du class. We rigorously characterize their inclusion relations and equivalence conditions. We then identify and analyze the rebound phenomenon in gradient iterations, whereby steps targeting low-frequency eigenvalues inadvertently amplify high-frequency gradient components. From this analysis, we derive the Hanoi ordering principle, which mandates recursive suppression of high-frequency components prior to each low-frequency step. This principle is further generalized to memory--m settings via a practical phase-wise recomputation strategy. Building on these foundations, we propose an adaptive Hanoi-like gradient method incorporating weighted component energy to select candidates and a settlement-continuation rule to compute the phase length for step reuse. We prove its global R-linear convergence for strictly convex quadratic objectives. Numerical experiments on ill-conditioned test problems demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms representative spectral gradient methods and the limited-memory BFGS method.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CBF方法在处理多个安全集交集时，逐点最小值运算会产生非光滑边界，标准Lie导数无法处理；现有的数学解决方法（如近似、概率非确定性、组合执行延迟）会引入偏差或阻碍硬件实时认证。

Method: 将系统状态和向量场嵌入到对偶数环（dual-number ring）中，通过单次评估同时提取复合势垒值及其精确方向导数；利用标准浮点最小值运算确定性地选择Clarke广义梯度中的单个顶点，该顶点被证明是一个有效的Clarke次梯度。该方法扩展至任意min/max布尔组合及高阶相对度系统。

Result: 该框架实现了同时强制执行的安全滤波器，保证了前向不变性；算术开销为固定常数因子，与状态维度或约束数量无关；通过三个物理实例验证了计算扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的对偶代数框架以确定性方式解决了CBF中非光滑边界的核心问题，无需近似或概率手段，且计算效率高，适用于硬实时硬件认证。

Abstract: This paper presents a dual-algebraic framework for control barrier functions (CBFs) that guarantees deterministic execution using exclusively elementary arithmetic. We develop this deterministic approach to solve a fundamental bottleneck in safety-critical control: pointwise minima naturally compose intersecting safe sets, but generate non-smooth boundaries where standard Lie derivatives fail. Existing mathematical workarounds inject approximation bias, probabilistic non-determinism, or combinatorial execution delays that strictly impede hard real-time hardware certification. By embedding the system state and vector field into the dual-number ring, our method extracts both the composite barrier value and its exact directional derivative in a single evaluation. The standard floating-point minimum deterministically isolates a single vertex of the Clarke generalized gradient for the quadratic-program solver. We prove this selected vertex constitutes a valid Clarke subgradient and the resulting simultaneous-enforcement safety filter guarantees forward invariance. The arithmetic overhead remains a fixed constant factor, strictly independent of state dimension or constraint count. We extend this framework to arbitrary $\min$/$\max$ Boolean compositions and systems of higher relative degree, validating the computational scaling on three physical examples.

</details>

*Lixin Tang,Xingyu Wang,Liwei Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对无约束弱凸问题的算法已达最优O(ε⁻³)样本复杂度，但处理含混合期望值约束的问题时，传统罚函数或增广拉格朗日方法需要繁琐的多循环结构，内循环复杂度高且参数调优敏感，存在理论空白。

Method: 提出单循环PD-HP算法，每步只需求解一个简单的强凸代理子问题并进行直接对偶投影；利用Halpern迭代加速和PAGE随机梯度估计技术；引入广义MFCQ和Hoffman误差界分析对偶乘子的稳定性。

Result: PD-HP算法达到了O(ε⁻³)的最优样本复杂度，匹配理论下界；在紧致多面体凸集约束下，对偶乘子具有确定性稳定性；复杂度界在标准无界KKT残差度量下成立，无需截断处理。

Conclusion: PD-HP算法以轻量级单循环结构实现了混合约束随机弱凸优化的最优样本复杂度，兼具高效性和易实现性，填补了该领域理论与算法之间的关键空白。

Abstract: We tackle the challenging problem of stochastic weakly convex optimization subject to mixed (equality and inequality) expected-value constraints. While optimal $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3})$ sample complexity algorithms exist for unconstrained weakly convex problems, dealing with complex functional constraints typically requires cumbersome multi-loop penalty or augmented Lagrangian methods, which suffer from high inner-loop complexity and sensitive parameter tuning. To bridge this fundamental gap, we propose the primal-dual Halpern-PAGE (PD-HP) algorithm. As a purely single-loop method, PD-HP completely bypasses the computational burden of nested iterations. At each step, it merely requires solving a simple strongly convex surrogate subproblem alongside a straightforward dual projection, making it exceptionally efficient and convenient to implement. Crucially, we prove that this computationally lightweight algorithm achieves the optimal $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-3})$ sample complexity for mixed-constrained stochastic weakly convex problems, successfully matching the theoretical lower bounds.
  Furthermore, when the primal domain is a compact polyhedral convex set, we establish the deterministic stability of the dual multipliers by exploiting the generalized Mangasarian-Fromovitz constraint qualification (MFCQ) alongside Hoffman's error bound. This ensures that our optimal complexity bound holds strictly under the standard, unbounded KKT residual metric without any theoretical gaps or artificial residual truncations.

</details>


### [82] [Learning Optimization Proxies for Sequential Contextual Stochastic Programs: An Order Fulfillment Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25362)
*Tinghan Ye,Shuaicheng Tong,Changkun Guan,Beste Basciftci,Pascal Van Hentenryck*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种场景嵌入神经网络代理（Scenario-Embedded Neural Network Proxy），通过离线训练和在线解码器结合，替代传统随机规划求解器，将全渠道订单履约问题的决策延迟降低约2800倍，同时实现履约成本降低3.3%（相较于C-SAA参考）和10.7%以上（相较于现有策略），并将延迟交付率减半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列上下文随机规划问题在实时决策系统中需要在不确定性下快速做出决策，但现有基于场景逼近的求解器虽然离线决策质量高，却需要数秒到数分钟的计算时间，无法满足高峰期亚秒级的响应需求。

Method: 提出学习型优化代理框架：场景嵌入神经网络（SENN）离线训练+在线可行性解码器。训练标签由上下文样本平均逼近（C-SAA）生成，复合损失函数包含标签模仿、约束违反惩罚和自监督成本对齐。专门应用于全渠道订单履约问题，构建了两阶段上下文随机规划模型。

Result: 在京东交易记录构建的校准模拟器中，代理将决策延迟降低约2800倍，履约成本比在线C-SAA参考降低3.3%，比现有履约策略降低至少10.7%，延迟交付率约减半。

Conclusion: 学习型优化代理能有效解决实时随机规划问题的计算瓶颈，在保证决策质量的同时实现亚秒级响应，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Sequential contextual stochastic programs model real-time decision systems in which each time epoch commits to an action under uncertainty whose consequences propagate into future decisions. In many practical contexts, these programs require obtaining solutions rapidly as new information becomes available. These problems can be represented through scenario approximations to be solved by off-the-shelf optimization solvers, which achieve high decision quality offline but typically run in seconds to minutes per instance, falling short of the sub-second responses that peak periods of planning require. This paper develops a learning-based optimization proxy: a scenario-embedded neural network trained offline on solver-generated labels, paired online with a decoder that enforces feasibility, replacing the per-epoch solve with a single forward pass. The framework is specialized to omnichannel order fulfillment, where each arriving order requires a sub-second assignment of products to distribution centers and carrier services under stochastic delivery times and future demand. A two-stage contextual stochastic program is introduced to formulate this problem, and its contextual sample average approximation (C-SAA) supplies the offline labels, while a composite training loss combines label imitation, a constraint-violation penalty, and self-supervised cost alignment. In a calibrated simulator built from JD.com transactional records, a detailed computational study is provided. The proxy reduces decision latency by roughly 2800x relative to the online finite-sample C-SAA reference and improves over it by 3.3% in realized fulfillment cost. Relative to established fulfillment policies, the proxy lowers total realized cost by at least 10.7% and roughly halves the late-delivery rate.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents a formalization of line search methods in the Lean 4 theorem prover. Our goal is to advance machine verification of nonlinear optimization theory by translating standard textbook definitions and convergence arguments into rigorous Lean code. We formalize fundamental notions related to gradient descent and descent directions, adaptive step-size selection via backtracking line search, and several classical line search criteria, including the Armijo, Goldstein, and Wolfe conditions, as well as nonmonotone variants. We further formalize a key convergence result, namely the Zoutendijk theorem, which plays a central role in the global convergence analysis of gradient-based iterative methods. By providing machine-checkable definitions and proofs for line search theory, this work complements existing formalizations of first-order optimization methods and establishes a foundation for the verified development of more advanced algorithms in nonlinear programming.

</details>


### [84] [Equilibrium singular dividend control under ambiguity aggregation of heterogeneous discount rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25461)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有异质性股东（不同贴现率）的公司在模糊态度下的最优分红问题，特别是时间不一致性带来的挑战。

Method: 通过验证定理提供均衡分红律的严格数学刻画，推导必要条件，并分析屏障型均衡的存在性与唯一性。

Result: 幂型和对数型聚合函数下屏障型均衡不存在；线性和指数聚合下存在且唯一；线性有界均衡收敛到奇异屏障型均衡。

Conclusion: 贴现率异质性和模糊厌恶显著影响均衡分红屏障，不同聚合函数会导致均衡存在性的根本差异。

Abstract: This paper studies a singular dividend control problem for a firm with heterogeneous shareholders whose discount rates follow a given distribution. The central planner aggregates expected discounted payoffs using an ambiguity aggregation function $phi$, which captures shareholder heterogeneity and ambiguity attitudes but also leads to time inconsistency. To address this issue, we seek a time-homogeneous equilibrium dividend law characterized by a partition of the state space into waiting and dividend-paying regions. We provide a rigorous mathematical characterization by proving a verification theorem and deriving necessary conditions for the equilibrium law. We then analyze barrier-type equilibria, showing non-existence for a class of aggregation functions that includes power-type and logarithmic aggregation functions, and establishing existence and uniqueness under linear and exponential aggregation. In the linear case, the bounded-rate equilibrium is shown to converge to the singular barrier-type equilibrium as the dividend rate bound tends to infinity. Numerical examples illustrate the effects of discount-rate heterogeneity and ambiguity aversion on the equilibrium barrier.

</details>

Abstract: This paper investigates transfer principles for generalized convexity of spectral functions on Euclidean Jordan algebras. A spectral function is induced by the eigenvalue map and an underlying symmetric function on a symmetric subset of $\mathbb{R}^n$. We establish transfer principles for several generalized convexity notions, such as (strict/semistrict) quasiconvexity and (strict) pseudoconvexity, together with their strong variants, by showing that these properties are preserved in both directions between a spectral function and its associated symmetric function. These results extend the transfer principles for (strict) convexity and provide a unified framework for generalized convexity in the setting of Euclidean Jordan algebras.

</details>


### [86] [Convergence of the Safeguarded Augmented Lagrangian Method under the Polyak-Lojasiewicz constraint qualification for Constrained Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25567)

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对目标函数为光滑与非光滑之和的约束复合优化问题，提出了带保护的增广拉格朗日方法（SALM）。在Polyak-Lojasiewicz约束规范（PLCQ）下证明了SALM收敛到M-稳定点的全局收敛性，并表明拉格朗日乘子的有界性依赖于非光滑部分的局部Lipschitz连续性。数值实验在稀疏投资组合优化问题上验证了理论，Lipschitz正则项表现显著优于非Lipschitz项，后者在很多实例中产生无界乘子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为约束复合优化问题（光滑+非光滑目标函数）提供具有理论保证的求解方法，并探究非光滑项Lipschitz连续性对算法稳定性的影响。

Method: 采用带保护的增广拉格朗日方法（SALM），在Polyak-Lojasiewicz约束规范（PLCQ）下分析收敛性，通过反例和数值实验验证非光滑项Lipschitz连续性的必要性。

Abstract: In this work we provide theoretical and practical results of the Safeguarded Augmented Lagrangian Method (SALM) for constrained composite optimization problems whose objective is the sum of a smooth and a nonsmooth function. We obtain global convergence results to an M-stationary point for SALM under the Polyak-Lojasiewicz constraint qualification (PLCQ). A counterexample shows that one cannot expect to get bounded multipliers without such an assumption. The performance of the algorithm is evaluated numerically on a set of sparse portfolio optimization problems with two different regularization terms, one being Lipschitz and the other one being non-Lipschitz. The results are significantly better for the Lipschitz sparsity term, whereas the underlying method generates seemingly unbounded multipliers in many instances when using the non-Lipschitz sparsity function.

Abstract: We present a method of ensuring recursive feasibility and stability for robust nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC) with multi-step predictors. Although feasibility guarantees are well-established for the case of single-step models applied recursively over a finite horizon, such guarantees are missing in naive MPC formulations that use distinct multi-step models to predict the system state at different future points in time. This issue arises because of potential inconsistencies in multi-step predictions generated at different times. Our approach checks for possible infeasibility of the robust nonlinear MPC optimisation problem \textit{a priori}, and uses information from previous solutions to provide a fall-back based on previously verified reachable sets. We illustrate the proposed predictor-substitution strategy with a simple numerical example.

</details>

Abstract: We present a Pontryagin-based numerical solver for deterministic optimal control problems in Bolza form. The solver regularizes the generally nonsmooth Hamiltonian using a log-sum-exp smoothing of a concave piecewise-affine bundle surrogate, yielding a smoothed Hamiltonian $H_δ$ that is $C^\infty$ and concave in the costate and $C^{1,1}$ in the state. The resulting two-point boundary value problem is discretized by a symplectic Euler scheme and solved by damped Newton iteration on the full-space nonlinear system in the discrete state and costate variables. A unified adaptive outer loop jointly controls the time-step distribution $Δt$, the number of planes in the bundle surrogate, and the smoothing parameter $δ$. The time-adaptation strategy follows the error-density framework of Karlsson, Larsson, Sandberg, and Tempone (2015). Related approaches include Pontryagin shooting, Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman methods, occupation-measure relaxations, max-plus approximations, and direct collocation. Numerical examples, including nonsmooth, singular, hypersensitive, and quadratic-programming-oracle benchmarks, demonstrate the roles of time refinement, bundle enrichment, and smoothing continuation. A linear--quadratic regulator with a closed-form Riccati solution is used as a calibration check.

</details>


### [90] [Convergence analysis of the Riemannian proximal gradient method with inexact oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25764)

TL;DR: 本文将不精确一阶预言机从欧几里得空间扩展至黎曼流形，提出黎曼近端梯度法(RPG-IO)，在温和条件下证明其全局收敛性，并基于KL性质建立完整迭代序列收敛性及显式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Riemannian优化方法通常假设精确的梯度/近端算子可用，但实际中往往只能获得近似计算（如子问题迭代求解、随机采样等），缺乏对不精确情形下收敛性的严格理论分析。

Method: 将不精确一阶预言机（满足加性或乘性误差界）推广至Riemannian流形，设计基于该预言机的Riemannian近端梯度法；利用Riemannian KL不等式建立全局收敛框架，按KL指数分情形推导收敛速率。

Result: (i)搜索方向范数收敛到零；(ii)函数值序列收敛到任意聚点处的函数值；(iii)迭代序列的每个聚点均为稳定点。在KL性质下，全序列收敛到唯一稳定点，并给出与KL指数相关的显式收敛速率；强不精确预言机下函数值以特定速率收敛到最优值。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地建立了Riemannian优化中不精确预言机的收敛理论，为非精确计算场景（如子问题近似求解、在线学习）提供了理论保证，填补了黎曼非凸优化中不精确一阶方法的分析空白。

Abstract: We extend the notion of an inexact first-order oracle from the Euclidean setting to Riemannian optimization, and conduct a convergence analysis for the Riemannian proximal gradient method equipped with this oracle, which we refer to as RPG-IO. Under mild conditions on the oracle errors, we establish the global convergence of RPG-IO. Specifically, we prove that (i) the norm of the search direction converges to zero; (ii) the sequence of function values converges to the function value at any accumulation point of the iterates; and (iii) every accumulation point of the iterates is a stationary point. Under the additional assumption of the Riemannian Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz (KL) property, we prove that the full sequence of iterates generated by RPG-IO converges to a single stationary point. Moreover, we derive explicit convergence rates when the KL exponent is specified. Finally, when a strong inexact oracle is employed, we establish the convergence rate of the sequence of function values to the optimal value.

</details>

Result: 该框架成功识别了不同决策管线之间的关键行为差异，展示了如何通过构建有针对性的假设性问题来揭示管线在宏观经济条件变化下的表现差异。

Conclusion: 该框架灵活通用，可支持针对特定投资组合目标的各种探询问题。将预测、优化和解释三者整合，能够产生更稳健、更透明的投资组合策略。

Abstract: We propose a predict-optimize-explain framework that uses gradient-based sample generation to interpret various portfolio models by identifying macroeconomic conditions that induce specified portfolio outcomes. Unlike traditional feature-importance methods, this approach directly probes decision pipelines (predictive models coupled with portfolio optimization) by constructing economically meaningful what-if questions. We focus on four such questions: under what macroeconomic conditions a predict-then-optimize pipeline closes or reverses its return gap with a predict-and-optimize pipeline; what conditions lead a pipeline to diversify rather than concentrate its allocation; when a pipeline trained on calm markets overtakes one trained through crises; and what conditions would let a pipeline match a benchmark return. These examples illustrate how our framework uncovers key behavioral differences between various decision pipelines. Beyond these cases, the proposed framework is flexible and can support a wide range of probing questions tailored to specific portfolio objectives. Our findings highlight the value of integrating prediction, optimization, and explanation to produce more robust and transparent portfolio strategies.

</details>


### [92] [A Methodology for Integrating Life Cycle Assessment into a Multidisciplinary Design Analysis and Optimization Framework for Sustainable Launcher Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25945)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将生命周期评估（LCA）集成到多学科设计分析与优化（MDAO）框架中的方法，用于运载火箭的早期设计阶段。通过参数化生命周期清单和轨迹优化，实现了性能与环境指标的多目标权衡分析，并以代表性一次性运载火箭为例进行了验证。

Method: 提出将LCA作为一个学科模块集成到MDAO框架中；建立依赖于设计变量和耦合变量的参数化生命周期清单，涵盖组件生产、推进剂生产和运输到发射场；基于优化后的轨迹剖面评估发射排放的气候变化影响；采用多目标优化方法分析性能与环境指标之间的权衡。

Result: 以代表性一次性运载火箭为例的多目标优化结果表明，不同环境影响类别之间存在拮抗行为，强调了在生态设计研究中审慎定义环境目标的重要性。

Conclusion: 该方法的通用性为在运载火箭早期设计阶段集成LCA奠定了基础，能够支持性能、成本与环境考量之间的权衡探索。

Abstract: The increasing number of orbital and sub-orbital launches makes it necessary to investigate the environmental impacts of launch vehicles and incorporate eco-design considerations into their development. In response, the European Space Agency has promoted Life Cycle Assessment (LCA) as a standardization methodology to mitigate environmental impacts of present and future space missions. This need is further amplified in the NewSpace, where numerous configurations and innovative technologies are explored, reinforcing the importance of integrating environmental considerations. At early design stages, launch vehicle architecture can be formalized through a multi-physics optimization problem based on Multidisciplinary Design Analysis and Optimization (MDAO) methods, where disciplines such as propulsion, aerodynamics, structure, and trajectory are coupled to obtain trade-offs among candidate configurations. This paper proposes a methodology to integrate an LCA discipline within an MDAO framework for launch vehicle design. The approach relies on parametric life-cycle inventories depending on design and coupling variables, covering component and propellant production as well as transport to the launch site. Launch emissions are evaluated from optimized trajectory profiles and characterized in terms of climate change impact. The methodology is illustrated on a representative expendable launch vehicle, where multi-objective optimizations assess trade-offs between performance and environmental indicators. Results highlight antagonistic behaviors among environmental impact categories, emphasizing the importance of carefully defining environmental objectives in eco-design studies. The generic nature of the methodology lays the foundation for integrating LCA into early-stage launch vehicle design, enabling exploration of trade-offs between performance, cost, and environmental considerations.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we devise a $\operatorname{prox}$-based semi-smooth Newton method that is applicable to a finite element discretization of a broad class of nonsmooth convex variational problems, including the TV-minimization problem, the $p$-Dirichlet problem, the obstacle problem, and the elasto-plastic torsion problem. To this end, on the basis of the proximity operator, the discrete primal-dual optimality conditions are reformulated as nonlinear operator equations with Newton-differentiable structure. Under suitable assumptions on the energy densities, we establish the global well-posedness and local super-linear convergence of the resulting semi-smooth Newton method. The proposed approach coincides with established semi-smooth Newton methods for obstacle-type problems, satisfies a primal-dual invariance, and, under suitable additional assumptions, is globally well-posed in the infinite-dimensional setting.

</details>


### [94] [Dominance-Based Feasibility Inference for Packing-Constrained Pickup and Delivery Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25970)

Main category: math.OC

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在2P-PDP中，货物的装载和卸载导致自由空间非单调演化，使得可行性检查计算复杂度过高，成为求解该问题的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Routing and packing are intrinsically coupled in transport problems, requiring joint planning for cost-efficient and physically realizable solutions. We study a pickup and delivery problem with two-dimensional packing constraints (2P-PDP). Unlike vehicle routing variants where items are loaded before vehicles leave the depot and packing is validated only once, the 2P-PDP induces non-monotonic free-space evolution, substantially increasing feasibility-checking complexity. To address this bottleneck, we propose a generic dominance-based feasibility framework that is embeddable in a broad class of exact and heuristic routing algorithms. Under no-relocation constraints, inferring feasibility from a previously verified packing state requires preserving the pickup and delivery order of onboard items. To this end, we introduce an order-preserving mapping that jointly captures geometric containment and sequence compatibility, enabling dominance-based inference by embedding the new packing state into a verified reference plan. To further reduce dominance-screening overhead, we design three search rules to guide candidate exploration and tailored strategies to store, retrieve, and prioritize verified states. Computational experiments show that the proposed approach reduces feasibility-checking time by up to 42% compared to a benchmark without dominance. The improvement stems from reducing exact packing-procedure calls, shifting verification effort away from the most computationally expensive stage.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统化框架，能将优化算法的紧致解析收敛证明（常通过计算机辅助获得）自动转化为等价的Lyapunov函数证明，并实现了一个结合PEP工具箱和基础线性代数的Jupyter notebook程序，用户无需理解实现细节即可为自己的算法搜索紧致的Lyapunov式证明；应用该框架发现了四个新的解析Lyapunov式证明，其中一个是强单调包含问题的新型精确最优近端算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Lyapunov函数证明方法依赖手工设计模板或对证明结构的先验洞察，且无法保证得到的Lyapunov分析能提供最紧致的收敛界。因此需要一种系统化的、能自动生成紧致Lyapunov证明的框架。

Method: 将性能估计问题（PEP）工具箱与基础线性代数相结合，构建一个将紧致解析收敛证明转化为Lyapunov函数证明的自动化流程，并以Jupyter notebook形式实现，用户可直接交互使用。

Result: 该框架成功复现了多个先前的Lyapunov分析；并发现了四个新的解析Lyapunov式证明，其中一个是强单调包含问题的新型精确最优近端算法。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个系统化、可交互的工具，使得优化算法的Lyapunov证明的搜索与验证变得自动化且保证紧致性，拓展了Lyapunov方法在优化算法分析中的应用边界。

Abstract: Lyapunov-style convergence proofs, which establish a nonincreasing sequence to provide a quantitative convergence rate for an algorithm, are popular and often considered desirable in first-order optimization. However, existing approaches to finding such Lyapunov functions rely on hand-designed templates or prior insight on the proof structure, and do not certify that the resulting Lyapunov-style analysis provides the sharpest convergence bound. In this work, we introduce a systematic framework for converting a tight, analytic convergence proof of an optimization algorithm, often found via computer assistance, into an equivalent proof based on Lyapunov functions. We implement a concrete procedure that combines a performance estimation problem (PEP) toolbox with elementary linear algebra, and show that it captures a number of prior Lyapunov analyses within a single Jupyter notebook. Based on our implementation, a user can straightforwardly test our systematic and interactive procedure on their own optimization algorithm of interest to search for a tight Lyapunov-style proof via code, without the need to comprehend the implementation details. We extend the application of our framework and discover four novel analytic Lyapunov-style proofs, where notably, one of them identifies a new exact optimal proximal algorithm for strongly monotone inclusion problems.

</details>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [The tilting property of Whittaker averaged central sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25387)
*Jeremy Taylor*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文用微局部方法描述了Iwahori-Whittaker平均的核，并将其应用于自守层，推广了Faergeman和Raskin关于反温和性等价于具有非正则奇异支撑的定理；同时利用Bezrukavnikov和Morton-Ferguson的Radon变换论证，将Whittaker平均中心层的倾斜性质推广到整数系数情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将反温和性与非正则奇异支撑的等价性定理进行推广，并进一步将Whittaker平均中心层的倾斜性质扩展到整数系数框架下。

Method: 利用微局部分析方法刻画Iwahori-Whittaker平均的核；采用Bezrukavnikov和Morton-Ferguson提出的Radon变换论证技术。

Result: 成功推广了Faergeman和Raskin的定理；将Whittaker平均中心层的倾斜性质从原有的系数情形推广到了整数系数。

Conclusion: 微局部方法有效揭示了Iwahori-Whittaker平均的核结构，为自守层理论提供了新的推广结果，并在整数系数下保持了倾斜性质。

Abstract: We characterize the kernel of Iwahori-Whittaker averaging in microlocal terms. Applying this to automorphic sheaves, we generalize Faergeman and Raskin's theorem that anti-temperedness is equivalent to having irregular singular support. Moreover, using a Radon transform argument of Bezrukavnikov and Morton-Ferguson, we extend the tilting property of Whittaker averaged central sheaves to integer coefficients.

</details>


### [97] [Total positivity and symmetric spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25516)
*Huanchen Bao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为对称空间 G/K 定义了全正性概念，通过取 Lusztig 全正部分 G_{>0} 在 G/K 中像的 Hausdorff 闭包实现。引入了对称空间的双 Bruhat 胞腔并定义其全正部分，证明了全非负对称空间的胞腔分解，给出了所有胞腔的显式正参数化，建立了闭包关系，并证明了两种自然参数化之间的转换映射是无减法的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 Lusztig 在一般线性代数群上提出的全正性理论推广到对称空间 G/K 这一更广泛的齐性空间上，建立对称空间的全正性框架。

Method: 通过 Hausdorff 闭包定义对称空间的全正性；引入双 Bruhat 胞腔结构；利用正参数化方法给出胞腔的显式表示；分析闭包关系与转换映射的代数性质。

Result: 建立了全非负对称空间的胞腔分解，给出了所有胞腔的显式正参数化，证明了闭包关系以及转换映射的无减法性质。

Conclusion: 成功将全正性理论从一般线性群推广至对称空间，建立了系统的胞腔分解与参数化理论，为相关领域（如表示论、组合学、可积系统）提供了新工具。

Abstract: We define a notion of total positivity for the symmetric space $G/K$ by taking the Hausdorff closure of the image of Lusztig's totally positive part $G_{>0}$ in $G/K$. We introduce double Bruhat cells for the symmetric space and define their totally positive pieces. We prove a cell decomposition of the totally nonnegative symmetric space, give explicit positive parametrizations of all cells, establish closure relations, and show that the transition maps between the two natural families of parametrizations are subtraction-free.

</details>


### [98] [Equivariant Cleft Extensions and Singular Equivalences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25560)
*Miltiadis Karakikes*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了阿贝尔范畴的裂扩张的等变提升及其对奇点范畴的影响，建立了将裂扩张提升为G-等变裂扩张的框架，并证明了裂扩张的限制函子诱导奇点等价当且仅当其等变版本也如此。作为应用，证明了G-等变θ-扩张的偏斜群环同构于基偏斜群环的\widehatθ-扩张，从而可将奇点等价提升到这些结构上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究阿贝尔范畴的裂扩张在等变框架下的提升行为，并探讨这种提升对奇点范畴等价关系的影响，旨在建立更广泛的奇点等价理论。

Method: 建立裂扩张到G-等变裂扩张的提升框架，通过比较限制函子与其等变版本的奇点等价性，并利用偏斜群环的同构关系实现具体应用。

Result: 裂扩张的限制函子诱导奇点等价当且仅当其等变对应版本也诱导奇点等价；G-等变θ-扩张的偏斜群环同构于基偏斜群环的\widehatθ-扩张。

Conclusion: 建立了等变框架下裂扩张与奇点等价之间的桥梁，为更复杂的代数结构中的奇点等价问题提供了有效的提升工具。

Abstract: We study the equivariant lifting of cleft extensions of abelian categories and its impact on singularity categories. Specifically, we establish the necessary framework for lifting a cleft extension to a G-equivariant cleft extension. Furthermore, we prove that a restriction functor associated to a cleft extension induces a singular equivalence if and only if its equivariant counterpart does. As a concrete application, we demonstrate that the skew group ring of a $G$-equivariant $θ$-extension is isomorphic to a $\widehatθ$-extension of the base skew group ring, allowing us to lift singular equivalences for these structures.

</details>


### [99] [The representation theory of the wreath product of a finite group with the monoid of all partial functions on a finite set as an EI-category algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25677)
*Itamar Stein*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文描述了有限群G与部分变换幺半群PT_n的圈积的复幺半群代数的通常箭图，其描述依赖于简单G-模张量积分解中的重数；同时证明了该代数的整体维数为n-1，并通过推广描述了G与保序部分函数子幺半群的圈积代数的箭图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画有限群G与部分变换幺半群PT_n的圈积的复幺半群代数的表示论结构，特别是其箭图和整体维数。

Method: 利用与该幺半群关联的Ehresmann EI-范畴进行分析，将代数结构问题转化为范畴表示问题。

Result: 给出了该代数的通常箭图描述，证明其整体维数为n-1，并推广到保序部分函数子幺半群的情形。

Conclusion: 通过Ehresmann EI-范畴方法成功描述了圈积幺半群代数的表示论性质，揭示了箭图结构与G-模张量积重数及整体维数与n的线性关系。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a finite group. We provide a description of the ordinary quiver of the complex monoid algebra of the wreath product $G \wr \mathrm{PT}_n$, where $\mathrm{PT}_n$ denotes the monoid of all partial functions on an $n$-element set. This description depends on the multiplicities of simple $G$-modules appearing in the decomposition of tensor products of simple $G$-modules. We also prove that the global dimension of this algebra is $n-1$. Both results are obtained by analyzing the associated Ehresmann EI-category related to the monoid. Finally, we describe the quiver of the algebra of the wreath product of $G$ with the submonoid of all order-preserving partial functions.

</details>


### [100] [Preprojective algebras and generalisations: A short survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25694)
*Aaron Chan,Osamu Iyama,Rene Marczinzik*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文综述了遗传代数H的预投射代数的两种推广形式——收缩预投射代数和完全预投射代数，并提出了若干开放问题以推动相关研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预投射代数在表示论中具有基础性地位，其通过Auslander-Reiten平移的轨道构造提供了研究遗传代数模结构的重要工具。然而，更一般的轨道代数尚未被充分探索。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，回顾了收缩预投射代数和完全预投射代数的定义与性质，并基于具体例子提出了多个开放问题和猜想。

Result: 系统梳理了两种推广预投射代数的框架，指出了当前理论中的空白，并列举了若干未解决的关键问题。

Conclusion: 收缩预投射代数和完全预投射代数扩展了经典预投射代数的理论，但仍有许多基本性质亟待研究；本文旨在通过开放问题激励关于H-模一般轨道代数的进一步探索。

Abstract: The preprojective algebra of a hereditary algebra $H$ can be defined as a certain orbit construction of the regular representation generated by the Auslander-Reiten translation. In this short survey, we will look at two important generalisations, namely, the contracted preprojective algebra and the total preprojective algebra. We will include several open problems and questions motivated by examples in the hope to stimulate future research on general orbit algebras of $H$-modules.

</details>


### [101] [Effective Computation of Mutation Paths and Generators of Cluster Automorphism Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25851)
*Jindong Zhao,Haiyan Zhu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Fu和Liang提出的标记顶点策略，设计了高效计算所有突变路径和簇自同构群生成元的方法，并应用于秩4有限突变型簇代数的簇自同构群生成元计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Fu和Liang的标记顶点策略，高效计算簇自同构群的生成元，解决现有方法在计算簇自同构群时效率不足的问题。

Method: 基于改进的标记顶点策略，设计算法高效计算所有突变路径和簇自同构群的生成元。

Result: 成功得到秩4有限突变型簇代数的簇自同构群的生成元。

Conclusion: 改进的标记顶点策略和算法能有效计算簇自同构群的生成元，特别适用于秩4有限突变型簇代数。

Abstract: In this paper, we improve the marked-vertex strategy introduced by Fu and Liang, and design the algorithm to compute all mutation paths and generator elements of cluster automorphism groups efficiently. As an application, we get generators of cluster automorphism groups of cluster algebras of finite mutation type of rank 4.

</details>

### [102] [Minimum-Weight Steiner Triangulation of Convex Polygons Requires Interior Steiner Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25302)
*David Eppstein,Zahra Hadizadeh*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个凸多边形，其最小权重斯坦纳三角剖分必须使用内部斯坦纳点，反驳了Eppstein于1994年提出的猜想——凸多边形的最小权重斯坦纳三角剖分仅需边界斯坦纳点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳Eppstein（1994）提出的猜想：凸多边形的最小权重斯坦纳三角剖分仅需边界斯坦纳点，无需内部斯坦纳点。

Method: 构造一个具体的凸多边形反例，证明其最小权重斯坦纳三角剖分必须引入内部斯坦纳点。

Result: 成功构造了一个凸多边形，其最小权重斯坦纳三角剖分必须使用内部斯坦纳点，而非仅依赖边界斯坦纳点。

Conclusion: Eppstein于1994年提出的猜想是错误的——凸多边形的最小权重斯坦纳三角剖分有时需要内部斯坦纳点。

Abstract: We construct a convex polygon for which the minimum-weight Steiner triangulation requires an interior Steiner point. This provides a counterexample to a 1994 conjecture of Eppstein that minimum-weight Steiner triangulation of convex polygons needs only Steiner points on the boundary of the polygon.

</details>


### [103] [Segment Watchman Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25816)
*Anna Brötzner,Omrit Filtser,Bengt J. Nilsson,Christian Rieck,Christiane Schmidt*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新的“分段警卫路线”问题，要求多边形内每一点都能被两条路线上的两点连线所覆盖，证明了在简单多边形中最小化最大路线长度是弱NP难问题，在带孔多边形中最小化总长度是NP难问题，并提出了多项式时间的2-近似算法以及充分条件，同时推广到k条路线的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受到鲁棒性守卫应用的启发，传统多警卫问题要求每个点至少被一个警卫看到，但无法保证从多个方向观测的鲁棒性。本文旨在解决每个点需从两个不同方向被看到的需求，即分段警卫路线问题。

Method: 定义分段警卫路线（segment watchman routes），证明在简单多边形中min-max准则的最优路线问题是弱NP-hard的（通过归约），在带孔多边形中min-sum准则的问题是NP-hard的；给出充分条件；设计多项式时间2-近似算法（同时适用于min-max和min-sum准则）；推广至k个警卫。

Result: 证明了min-max准则在简单多边形中弱NP-hard，min-sum准则在带孔多边形中NP-hard；给出了充分条件和多项式时间2-近似算法；结果可推广到k个警卫。

Conclusion: 本文系统研究了分段警卫路线问题的计算复杂性，确立了其难解性边界，同时提供了实用的近似算法，为鲁棒性守卫应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Motivated by applications for robust guarding, we consider a variant of the multiple-watchmen problem that ensures that every point within a polygon $P$ is seen from more than one direction: we search for two routes $W_1,W_2$, such that every point $p\in P$ is contained in a segment $\overline{w_1w_2}\subseteq P$ such that $w_1\in W_1$ and $w_2\in W_2$. We call such routes segment watchman routes.
  We show that finding the two routes that are optimal with respect to the min-max criterion is weakly NP-hard even in simple polygons, and that finding the routes that are optimal with respect to the min-sum criterion is NP-hard in polygons with holes. Moreover, we present sufficient conditions for routes to be segment watchman routes, and provide a polynomial-time $2$-approximation under both the min-max criterion and the min-sum criterion, both in simple polygons. Finally, we show how to generalize our results for $k$ watchmen.

</details>


### [104] [Sharp approximate Carathéodory theorem and application to iterated Delaunay refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25854)
*Raphaël Tinarrage*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文分析了球面Delaunay复形在迭代细化过程中单纯形直径的收缩性，证明了Delaunay细化比传统细分方法具有更强的收缩效果，并通过Maurey经验方法的强化和近似Carathéodory定理建立了严格的收缩界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对Delaunay复形细化的收缩性质缺乏严格的理论分析；与传统细分不同，细化后的Delaunay复形不一定是前一个的细分，因此网格收缩不是自动成立的，需要严格证明。

Method: 将问题简化为欧几里得单纯形的紧覆盖估计；通过枢轴抽样强化Maurey经验方法，并结合维度依赖版本的近似Carathéodory定理来获得这些估计。

Result: 推导了若干Steiner点族（包括重心细分和边细分对应的Delaunay类似物）的显式收缩界；理论和数值实验均表明Delaunay细化比其细分对应物实现更强的收缩。

Conclusion: Delaunay细化在网格收缩方面优于传统细分方法，其理论基础为单纯形直径的收缩提供了严格保证。

Abstract: We analyze the decrease of simplex diameters under iterated refinement of spherical Delaunay complexes. Unlike in ordinary subdivision, the refined Delaunay complex need not be a subdivision of the previous one, so mesh contraction is not automatic. We derive explicit contraction bounds for several families of Steiner points, including Delaunay analogues of barycentric and edgewise subdivision. The proof reduces the problem to sharp covering estimates for Euclidean simplices. These estimates are obtained through a strengthening of Maurey's empirical method via pivotal sampling and a dimension-dependent version of the approximate Carathéodory theorem. Theoretical results and numerical experiments show that Delaunay refinements achieve stronger contraction than their subdivision counterparts.

</details>


### [105] [Furthest Pair Requires Quadratic Time in Superconstant Dimension under SETH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25887)
*Barna Saha,Yinzhan Xu,Christopher Ye*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在SETH假设下，对于所有有效可构造的维度d=ω(1)，计算几何中若干基本问题（如最远点对、双色最近点对、最大内积、Hopcroft问题）需要n^{2-o(1)}时间，表明已知算法对维度的依赖本质上是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算几何中多个基本问题存在运行时间为f(d)·n^{2-Θ(1/d)}的算法，但关于维度d对下界的影响尚不清晰。Chen（2020）仅在d=2^{Θ(log^* n)}时证明了n^{2-o(1)}的下界，本文希望将该下界推广到所有有效可构造的维度d=ω(1)。

Method: 利用OpenAI最近否定Erdos单位距离猜想的证明技术（由ChatGPT 5.5 Pro初步发现，作者验证并大量编辑完善），将Chen的下界从特定维度扩展到所有有效可构造的维度d=ω(1)。

Result: 成功将n^{2-o(1)}下界从d=2^{Θ(log^* n)}扩展到所有有效可构造的维度d=ω(1)，即只要维度趋于无穷，这些问题的下界就成立。

Conclusion: 在SETH假设下，计算几何中这些经典问题的已知算法对维度的依赖在渐近意义下是最优的，维度带来的加速（f(d)因子）无法被本质改进。

Abstract: Several fundamental problems in computational geometry admit algorithms with running time $f(d) \cdot n^{2-Θ(1/d)}$ for $n$ points in $d$ dimensions, making them among the most prominent examples of barely subquadratic computation. Notable members of this class include Furthest Pair, Bichromatic Closest Pair, (Bichromatic) Maximum Innter Product, and Hopcroft's Problem. Chen [Theory Comput. 2020] proved that, assuming the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH), these problems require $n^{2-o(1)}$ time when the dimension satisfies $d=2^{Θ(\log^* n)}$. We extend this lower bound to all efficiently constructible dimensions $d=ω(1)$. Thus, assuming SETH, the dependence of the best known algorithms on the dimension is essentially unavoidable. The proof utilizes techniques in OpenAI's recent disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture.
  The proof was initially discovered by ChatGPT 5.5 Pro. The authors have validated and substantially edited the proof to improve the presentation.

</details>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [X-rays Mark the Spot: The Effects of Reduced Metallicity on X-ray AGN Obscuration at High Redshift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25018)
*Yash A. Gursahani,Christopher S. Reynolds*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用蒙特卡洛方法模拟高红移（z=10） obscured AGN的X射线辐射传输，发现低金属丰度可显著增加X射线光子逃逸概率，提高探测前景；遮蔽几何结构通过多次散射与几何束流效应复杂影响逃逸率；并评估了下一代高分辨率X射线巡天对Compton厚AGN的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JWST将高红移星系和AGN观测推进到z>10，对应宇宙大爆炸后5亿年内的早期超大质量黑洞种子形成与增长阶段，这些黑洞可能处于高度遮蔽环境。由于早期宇宙预测存在显著较低的铁丰度，当前尚不清楚这如何影响遮蔽AGN的X射线性质，因此需要系统研究。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛方法模拟来自中心AGN的X射线在周围冷气体环状（torus）结构中的辐射传输过程，表征出射X射线谱随金属丰度、环状体张角和柱密度的变化。重点关注Compton厚遮蔽（柱密度N_H=10^24-10^25 cm^-2）区域，并探索非太阳丰度比（模拟Ia型超新星延迟时间分布的影响）。

Result: 降低金属丰度可显著增加X射线光子从环状体中逃逸的比例，提升高红移AGN的探测前景；遮蔽覆盖因子（张角）通过环状体内部多次散射（各向同性化）与开口逃逸（几何束流效应）的竞争发挥复杂作用。

Conclusion: 低金属丰度改善极高红移遮蔽AGN的X射线可探测性，模型可用于评估下一代高角分辨率X射线巡天对z=10高度遮蔽AGN的探测能力。

Abstract: The James Webb Space Telescope has pushed the frontier of high-redshift galaxy and active galactic nucleus (AGN) observations firmly past $z=10$. Corresponding to the first 500 Myr after the Big Bang, this coincides with the epoch of supermassive black hole seeding and their early growth, much of which is likely to occur in highly obscured environments. In this work, we investigate the expected X-ray properties of these obscured AGNs focusing on the impact of the significantly lower iron abundance predicted at such early times. We use Monte Carlo methods to model the radiative transfer of X-rays from a central AGN through a surrounding torus of cold gas, characterizing the emergent X-ray spectrum as a function of the metallicity, opening angle of the torus, and column density. Motivated by expectations of high-$z$ systems, we focus on Compton-thick obscurers with columns $N_H=10^{24}-10^{25}\,{\rm cm}^{-2}$. We find that decreased metallicity can significantly increase the fraction of X-ray photons that escape the torus, improving the prospects of detecting these very high-$z$ AGNs. The covering fraction of the obscurer (i.e. torus opening angle) plays a complex role, with repeated scatterings across the interior of the torus (isotropizing the emission) competing with escape through the opening, producing geometric beaming. Additionally, we explore non-solar abundance ratios that mimic the delay-time distribution of Type Ia supernovae. We use our models to address the detectability of highly obscured $z=10$ AGNs in next-generation, high-angular resolution X-ray surveys.

</details>


### [107] [Multi-messenger and Multi-band Studies of Massive Black Holes: the Synergies Between LISA and SKAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25020)
*Pedro R. Capelo,Alberto Mangiagli,Lorenz Zwick,Lucio Mayer,Marta Volonteri*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了嵌入气体的超大质量双黑洞（BH）系统，因质量不同可同时在nHz和mHz频段发射引力波（GW）及全波段电磁波（EM）。SKAO与LISA的联合观测有望实现首个同源多波段（nHz-mHz）引力波探测以及GW-EM（mHz-射电）协同探测。通过一致的BH双星种群模拟，发现LISA源的射电对应体数量对喷流/耀发模型及SKA-Mid配置不敏感。此外，SKAO作为脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）的一部分，与LISA可探测同一目标的首个低频多波段引力波信号；气体盘中的超大质量BH双星因流体动力学力矩产生高频谐波，主载波可被SKAO-era PTA识别为确定性信号，高频谐波则在LISA中表现为随机信号，多波段关联将为最质量BH的环境提供前所未有的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用SKAO与LISA的联合观测能力，实现同源多波段（nHz-mHz）引力波探测及GW-EM协同探测，并通过一致的BH双星种群模拟，评估不同射电模型对可探测信号数量的影响，进而利用多波段引力波关联约束超大质量BH的周边环境。

Method: 基于数值模拟和模拟星表，采用一致的BH双星种群模型，对比不同射电耀发/喷流模型对LISA源射电对应体数量的影响；同时利用SKAO作为PTA与LISA协同，分析nHz-mHz多波段引力波信号的特征（主载波为确定性信号，高频谐波为随机信号）。

Result: LISA源的射电对应体数量对喷流/耀发模型的选择以及SKA-Mid是AA*还是AA4配置不敏感；SKAO-era PTA可识别主载波为确定性信号，LISA可探测高频谐波为随机信号，有望实现首个同源多波段引力波探测。

Conclusion: SKAO与LISA的联合观测将在多波段引力波天文学中发挥关键作用，实现GW-EM和GW-GW协同探测，并通过多波段引力波关联为超大质量BH的环境提供前所未有的约束。

Abstract: Depending on its mass, the same gas-embedded massive black hole (BH) binary can emit gravitational waves (GWs) in one or more bands (nHz and mHz) concurrently, along with electromagnetic (EM) waves over the entire spectrum. The Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO), thanks to its unparalleled sensitivity, will be pivotal in achieving coincident GW-EM (mHz-radio) and GW-GW (nHz-mHz) detections. We review the state-of-the-art predictions - achieved by means of numerical simulations and mock catalogues - of the numbers of detectable coincident GW-EM signals when employing the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) and SKA-Mid. By exploiting the same underlying BH binary populations, to allow for a fairer comparison, we then assess the importance of a variety of EM models for the radio flares and jets, finding that the number of radio counterparts of LISA sources is relatively insensitive to the jet/flare model employed and to whether SKA-Mid AA* or AA4 is assumed. Additionally, we describe how SKAO - as part of a pulsar timing array (PTA) - and LISA will provide the opportunity to detect the first low-frequency, multi-band (nHz-mHz) GW detection of the same object. Supermassive BH binaries embedded in gas discs are subjected to hydrodynamical torques, causing perturbations that produce additional small-amplitude, higher-frequency GWs. The main carrier GWs may thus be identifiable as a deterministic signal by SKAO-era PTAs, while the higher-frequency harmonics would shine in LISA as stochastic signals. Correlating these multi-band GWs would provide unprecedented constraints on the environment of the most massive BHs.

</details>


### [108] [Formation of Black Hole-White Dwarf X-ray Binaries in Globular Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25030)
*William Y. W. Yang,Kyle Kremer,James C. Lombardi,,Kristen C. Dage*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过Cluster Monte Carlo动力学代码模拟，研究了球状星团中黑洞+白矮星超致密双星系统的形成机制，发现黑洞与巨星碰撞（共同包层事件）是主要形成途径。这些系统是X射线源和毫赫兹引力波源，可作为LISA的探测目标，预计银河系内约有一个可被LISA分辨的源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球状星团中存在大量动力学活跃的恒星遗迹，这些遗迹与多种天体物理源相关。研究超致密双星（恒星质量黑洞吸积白矮星伴星）的形成机制，有助于理解这类可作为多信使观测目标（X射线+引力波）的天体系统。

Method: 使用Cluster Monte Carlo动力学代码进行模拟，研究超致密双星形成；利用平滑粒子流体动力学代码StarSmasher模拟黑洞+巨星碰撞的"共同包层"事件结果；构建本地超致密X射线源模拟星表，并与银河系和河外星系候选源对比；计算引力波应变。

Result: 黑洞与巨星的碰撞是形成黑洞+白矮星超致密双星的主要机制；碰撞产生的双星通过引力波旋近在亚Gyr时间尺度内进入罗氏接触；银河系内约有一个源可被LISA分辨。

Conclusion: 球状星团中的黑洞+白矮星超致密双星是LISA的潜在探测目标，可为观测球状星团中新的黑洞提供有力工具。

Abstract: Globular clusters are host to significant populations of dynamically-active stellar remnants that connect to a variety of astrophysical sources. Using simulations performed with the Cluster Monte Carlo dynamics code, we study the formation of ultracompact binaries in which a stellar-mass black hole accretes material from a white dwarf companion in a sub-hour orbit. These binary systems are prime multimessenger targets, as they can be observed as both luminous X-ray sources, and as millihertz gravitational wave sources detectable by the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). We find that black hole+giant collisions are the primary mechanism through which such systems form. We model the outcomes of these ``common envelope''-like events using the smoothed particle hydrodynamics code StarSmasher, and verify these collisions yield black hole+white dwarf binaries that enter Roche contact on sub-Gyr timescales via gravitational wave inspiral. We construct a mock catalog of local ultracompact X-ray sources and compare to candidate sources observed in globular clusters in the Milky Way (e.g., 47 Tuc X9) and external galaxies (e.g., RZ 2109 in NGC 4472). Finally, we compute the gravitational wave strain for these sources, and show that of order one source may be resolvable in the Milky Way by LISA, representing a potentially powerful tool for observing new black holes in globular clusters.

</details>


### [109] [The kinetic-energy bottleneck in Fast Radio Burst models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25035)
*Paz Beniamini,Pawan Kumar*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文对快速射电暴（FRB）的三种主要模型（内磁层模型、光柱尺度强制重联模型、外部激波脉泽模型）进行了模型无关的辐射机制约束分析，通过推导亮度温度、动光度等约束条件，推断发射区的密度、尺寸和粒子洛伦兹因子。结论认为，在持续粒子加速条件下，中子星表面附近的磁层模型仍是最有前景的FRB发射机制，但需克服波成功逃离磁层的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数FRB模型涉及"引擎释放能量→转化为粒子动能→辐射相干GHz辐射"的两步过程，但缺乏对发射机制的统一约束。本文旨在推导模型无关的约束条件，系统评估三类主流FRB模型的可行性。

Method: 通过推导亮度温度约束和动光度约束等模型无关条件，推断发射区密度、尺寸和粒子洛伦兹因子；将约束应用于三类FRB模型（内磁层、光柱尺度重联、外部激波脉泽）进行定量可行性评估。

Result: （i）内磁层模型：除非粒子被持续原地加速，否则违反亮温度与动光度约束；怪兽激波方案虽提供持续加速，但要求粒子密度超过Goldreich-Julian值约10^12倍，将脉泽峰值移至约10^3 GHz以上；（ii）光柱尺度强制重联：提供持续加速，但重联层辐射的射电能量仅为注入能量的约10^-6；（iii）外部激波脉泽：满足约束，但上游风对受激康普顿散射不透明，提出的逃逸路径（发射高于脉泽峰或上游磁化参数σ_w≥30）导致极低效率，前者还与窄谱矛盾。

Conclusion: 持续粒子加速的中子星表面附近磁层模型仍是最有前景的FRB发射场景，但需解决波从磁层成功逃离的问题（详见引言部分）。

Abstract: Most Fast Radio Burst (FRB) models invoke a two-step process in which energy released by the central engine is converted into particle kinetic energy and only subsequently radiated as coherent GHz emission. We derive model-independent constraints on FRB emission mechanisms and use them to infer the density, size, and particle Lorentz factor of the emitting region. We assess the implications for the three main classes of FRB models. (i) Inner-magnetospheric models violate brightness-temperature and kinetic-luminosity constraints unless particles are continuously re-accelerated in situ. Magnetar-strength magnetic fields can supply the required parallel electric field out to $R\lesssim10^{10} \mathrm{cm}$ with additional, model-dependent constraints. The monster-shock scenario provides such continuous acceleration, but requires particle densities exceeding the Goldreich-Julian value by $\gtrsim 10^{12}$, shifting the maser peak to $\gtrsim10^3$ GHz for typical FRB luminosities. (ii) Light-cylinder-scale forced-reconnection provides continuous particle acceleration but the radio energy emitted from the compressed reconnection layer is typically only $\lesssim10^{-6}$ of the injected energy. (iii) External-shock maser models satisfy kinetic-luminosity and brightness-temperature constraints. However, we show that the upstream wind is unavoidably optically thick to induced Compton scattering, independent of the model's principal parameters. Proposed escape routes - emission above the maser peak or upstream magnetization $σ_{\rm w}\gtrsim30$ - lead to tiny efficiencies, while the former also conflicts with narrow FRB spectra. We conclude that magnetospheric models operating near the neutron-star surface and incorporating continuous particle acceleration remain the most promising FRB emission scenario, subject to successful wave escape from the magnetosphere (discussed in the Introduction).

</details>
