Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 微类星体GRS 1915+105的LHAASO和Fermi-LAT联合分析显示，其伽马射线发射中心相对于双星系统偏移约0.13°，能谱呈对数抛物线型且无尖锐截断，表明质子加速能量超过5 PeV，喷流机械功率中至少有几个百分比转移给了质子，证实微类星体是银河系中高效的粒子加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微类星体作为拥有相对论性喷流的双星系统，被认为是超越PeV能量级的粒子加速源，但此前缺乏对这类源的高能伽马射线辐射的详细观测证据，尤其是对GRS 1915+105这一典型微类星体的宽带能谱和空间分布的系统研究。

Method: 基于LHAASO（4年数据，含WCDA和KM2A子阵列）和Fermi-LAT（17年数据）的观测数据，对GRS 1915+105进行联合分析，包括空间扩展性分析、能谱拟合（采用对数抛物线函数）以及辐射机制建模。

Result: 发现扩展伽马射线发射中心偏移约0.13°；能谱呈对数抛物线型渐近陡化，无尖锐截断；GeV至PeV能段呈单一幂律谱；模型拟合表明质子最大能量超过5 PeV，喷流机械功率中至少几个百分比转移给了质子。

Conclusion: 研究结果加强了微类星体作为银河系中极端高效粒子加速器的证据，表明其加速效率接近源内可用电势降所允许的极限。

Abstract: Microquasars, binary systems featuring relativistic jets, have emerged as sources for particle acceleration beyond PeV energies. We present a study of the broadband $γ$-ray emission from one of the most prominent Galactic microquasars GRS 1915+105 based on data accumulated by LHAASO and Fermi-LAT over 4 and 17 years, respectively. A joint analysis of LHAASO-WCDA and LHAASO-KM2A data reveals extended $γ$-ray emission whose centroid appears significantly shifted, by ~ 0.13°, from the binary system and its jets. The spectral energy distribution is well described by a curved spectrum with progressive steepening that can be described by a log-parabola function with no evidence for a sharp cutoff, consistent with parent particles reaching multi-PeV energies and an extreme acceleration efficiency approaching the limit set by the available potential drop across the source. Several features, most notably the shift of the emission and single-power-law spectrum down to GeV band, favor radiation by cosmic rays accelerated in the source interacting with the dense ambient medium. Our spectral modeling implies that at least a few percent of the jet mechanical power is transferred to protons, whose maximum energy reaches beyond 5 PeV. These results strengthen the case for microquasars as exceptionally efficient accelerators in our Galaxy.

</details>


### [111] [3D particle-in-cell simulations of pulsar wind-disk interaction: application to the transitional millisecond pulsar PSR J1023+0038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25114)
*Valentina Richard-Romei,Benoît Cerutti,Alessandro Papitto,Riccardo La Placa*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过三维粒子网格模拟，研究了脉冲星风与吸积盘相互作用对磁层结构、等离子体动力学及同步辐射的影响，成功解释了过渡毫秒脉冲星PSR J1023+0038在亚发光盘状态下的光学和X射线脉冲辐射特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚缺乏从第一性原理出发的模型来描述脉冲星风与吸积盘相互作用，以解释过渡毫秒脉冲星在亚发光盘状态下观测到的宽波段光变和X射线脉冲特征。

Method: 采用三维粒子网格模拟，构建脉冲星磁层与完美导电环（模拟吸积盘）相互作用的模型，分析磁重联、等离子体加速及同步辐射过程。

Result: 吸积盘导致磁层显著重构，内盘边界等离子体密度、磁场强度增加，等离子体各向同性化和粒子加速效率提高；同步辐射增强，光变曲线呈单峰或双峰结构；偏振度降低且具有能量依赖性，偏振角旋转模式依赖于观测视角。

Conclusion: 模型成功再现了PSR J1023+0038的光学和X射线脉冲辐射主要特征，支持这些脉冲辐射起源于脉冲星风与吸积盘内缘相互作用的同步辐射机制。

Abstract: Transitional millisecond pulsars constitute a peculiar subclass of neutron stars in which the pulsar alternates between accretion-powered and rotation-powered states, depending on the variations in the mass accretion flow coming from a low-mass companion star. A third intermediate state, referred to as ``sub-luminous disk state'', has been identified. During this state, observations indicate the presence of a disk surrounding the pulsar, and the system exhibits intriguing features, such as broad optical and X-ray pulsations characterized by a high luminosity. To date, no ab initio model of a pulsar wind interacting with an accretion disk has been developed to address these observables. We perform three-dimensional particle-in-cell simulations of a pulsar magnetosphere surrounded by a perfectly conducting torus to model the interaction between the pulsar wind and the disk. We find that the presence of the disk induces a significant reconfiguration of the magnetosphere compared to the rotation-powered state, leading to enhanced plasma density at the inner disk boundary, increased magnetic field strength, and more efficient plasma isotropization and particle acceleration. As a result, the synchrotron radiation is substantially enhanced, and characterized by a strong continuous component and either one or two-peaked light curves, depending on the pulsar's magnetic obliquity. The polarization degree is reduced compared to isolated systems, and its energy dependence is explored. The rotation of the polarization angle can also be altered, depending on the observer's viewing angle. The model successfully reproduces some of the main features of the optical and X-ray pulsed emission originating from PSR J1023+0038, thereby corroborating the scenario in which these pulsations originate from synchrotron radiation generated as the pulsar wind interacts with the inner edge of the disk.

</details>


### [112] [Unique Science Opportunities for Space VLBI Systems with the SKA Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25137)
*Y. Y. Kovalev,G. Bruni,T. An,H. E. Bignall,P. G. Edwards,C. Garcia-Miro,M. Giroletti,L. I. Gurvits,M. Kadler,J. -Y. Kim,E. V. Kravchenko,I. Liodakis,Y. Q. Liu,A. V. Plavin,A. V. Popkov,A. B. Pushkarev,T. Savolainen,Z. -Q. Shen,A. Tamar,E. Traianou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用SKA-Mid作为地面锚点进行下一代空间甚长基线干涉测量（SVLBI）的前景，涵盖从AGN喷流物理、高能中微子源搜索到超精密天体测量等多个前沿科学方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RadioAstron等任务在耀变体中发现的极端亮温度超越了现有理论预测，亟需更高灵敏度的下一代SVLBI来深入探究粒子（再）加速机制，这对理解高能中微子来源具有直接意义。同时，现有SVLBI在灵敏度、红移覆盖及天体测量精度方面仍存在局限。

Method: 文章提出以SKA-Mid相控阵形成的多波束作为地面锚点，结合未来空间VLBI卫星构建厘米波SVLBI系统；与毫米波地面VLBI协同实现多波段匹配分辨率观测；利用MultiView技术实现超精密天体测量。

Result: 预期成果包括：（1）探测极高红移AGN并克服（1+z）移位导致的星等吸收问题；（2）通过法拉第旋转测绘研究喷流形成、加速、准直及磁场演化；（3）实现脉冲星和巨脉泽的河外视差测量、超大质量黑洞的自行测量，以及系外行星的天体测量探测。

Conclusion: SKA-Mid作为下一代SVLBI地面锚点，将开辟角分辨率-灵敏度参数空间的前沿领域，推动从近邻恒星系外行星到早期宇宙AGN演化的全尺度天体物理研究。

Abstract: To date, two dedicated Space Very Long Baseline Interferometry (SVLBI) missions, the VLBI Space Observatory Programme (VSOP) and RadioAstron, have provided groundbreaking insights into the Universe at angular resolutions as fine as ~10 microarcseconds. The phased SKA-Mid, with its exceptional sensitivity and broad frequency coverage, will form a unique ground-based anchor for future SVLBI missions, driving major advances into previously unexplored regions of the angular resolution-sensitivity parameter space. The discovery of extreme brightness temperatures in blazars by RadioAstron demands detailed investigation with next-generation SVLBI. Such studies are crucial for understanding particle (re-)acceleration mechanisms, with direct implications for the search for high-energy neutrino sources. Combining centimeter-wavelength SVLBI with millimeter ground-based VLBI at comparable resolutions will enable detailed studies of plasma stratification and instabilities in Active Galactic Nuclei (AGN) jets, as well as the processes of jet formation, acceleration, collimation, and magnetic field evolution, for example through Faraday rotation mapping. The unprecedented sensitivity of the SKA-Mid will allow observations of active galactic nuclei to very high redshifts, tracing their evolution and overcoming opacity caused by the (1+z) shift of intrinsic emission frequencies. Future centimeter SVLBI experiments will also probe scattering in the interstellar medium through pulsar, maser, and AGN observations. Finally, the combination of multiple tied-array beams from the SKA telescopes and the extremely long SVLBI baselines will enable ultra-precise astrometry using the next-generation MultiView technique, allowing measurements of extragalactic parallaxes of pulsars and megamasers, proper motions of supermassive black holes, and even the astrometric detection of exoplanets.

</details>


### [113] [Symbiotic Magnetogenesis during Radiation Domination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25171)
*Stephon Alexander,Ariel Marxena Baksh,Lawrence Edmond,Wenrong Sun*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出了一种晚期宇宙磁产生机制，通过耦合的轴子-膨胀子系统在暗U(1)规范场中产生磁场。膨胀子的指数耦合重塑不稳定性带驱动放大，轴子控制螺旋性结构，两者共同产生中等手性的暗磁场，无需精细调节。数值演化显示在模糊暗物质和超轻轴子参数范围内均有效，基准案例在1 Mpc尺度产生约0.9 nG的磁场，并通过动力学混合传递到可见区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种晚期宇宙中无需精细调节即可产生具有中度手性的宇宙磁场的机制，同时避免可见区等离子体电导率对磁场放大的抑制。

Method: 利用耦合的轴子-膨胀子系统（膨胀子提供指数耦合驱动不稳定性放大，轴子控制手性结构）在暗U(1)规范场中放大磁场。从红移z=10^5到物质-辐射等时进行数值演化，并对轴子质量、膨胀子初始条件和耦合强度进行参数搜索。

Result: 该机制在模糊暗物质和超轻轴子参数范围内均能产生显著的磁场放大。基准案例在约1 Mpc尺度产生约0.9 nG的磁场，且可通过动力学混合高效传递到可见区。

Conclusion: 该机制能够在暗区产生中度手性的宇宙磁场，无需对任一标量区进行精细调节，且在广泛的参数空间内保持稳健，为宇宙磁场的起源提供了新的可行方案。

Abstract: We present a late-time magnetogenesis mechanism in which a coupled axion-dilaton system sources a dark $U(1)$ gauge field. The dilaton's exponential coupling drives tachyonic amplification by reshaping the instability band, while the axion controls the helicity structure of the field. Together, they amplify both gauge helicities and produce a moderately chiral dark magnetic field without fine-tuning in either scalar sector. The field is generated in the dark sector, thus the mechanism avoids plasma-conductivity suppression in the visible sector, while the model remains robust across a broad range of scalar-masses and couplings. Numerical evolution from $z=10^5$ to matter-radiation equality, combined with a parameter search over the axion mass, dilaton initial conditions, and dilaton coupling, shows that astrophysically relevant amplification persists across fuzzy-dark-matter and ultralight-axion regimes. A benchmark case yields $B\approx0.9 ~\mathrm{nG}$ on $λ_0\sim1 \mathrm{Mpc}$, with kinetic mixing transferring the field to the visible sector over a broad range of mixing parameters.

</details>


### [114] [Causality alone bounds the maximum radius difference between different-mass neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25186)
*Aleksi Kurkela,Tuhin Malik*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用因果性条件和手征有效场论在饱和密度附近的约束，推导出中子星不同质量半径之间的解析上限，并据此对NICER观测数据进行了无模型依赖的重新分析，仅保留7.5%的联合后验分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究共同因果状态方程（EoS）的假设如何关联不同质量中子星的半径，从而减少独立观测推断的不确定性，并希望提供一种独立于具体EoS先验的透明基准来解释中子星观测。

Method: 基于因果性条件和手征有效场论（χEFT）在饱和密度附近的EoS约束，推导出半径差异的闭合形式上界：R(2.0M⊙) ≤ 1.16 R(1.4M⊙) - 1.1 km。该界可通过一族解析构造的单参数EoS饱和。将此因果上限作为先验独立约束施加于NICER对PSR J0437-4715、PSR J0614-3329和PSR J0740+6620的后验分布上。

Result: 该因果上限去除了PSR J0740+6620后验的大半径尾部，仅保留7.5%的联合乘积分布。

Conclusion: 该方法与完整EoS信息推断不同，它清晰地隔离了通用物理假设（共同因果EoS）的后果与特定EoS先验选择带来的影响，为解释中子星观测提供了一个透明的基准。

Abstract: We investigate how the assumption of a common causal equation of state (EoS) correlates the radii of neutron stars at different masses and thereby reduces the uncertainties inferred from independent observations. We show that causality, anchored only to the chiral effective field theory ($χ$EFT) EoS near saturation density, places a closed-form upper bound on the radius difference, $R(2.0\,M_\odot)\le 1.16\,R(1.4\,M_\odot)-1.1\,$km. The bound is saturated exactly by a one-parameter family of EoSs that we construct analytically. Imposing this prior-independent causal ceiling on the independent NICER posteriors of PSR J0437-4715, PSR J0614-3329, and PSR J0740+6620 retains only 7.5% of their joint product distribution and removes the large-radius tail of the PSR J0740+6620 posterior. Unlike full EoS-informed inferences, our construction cleanly isolates the consequences of the generic physical assumptions of a common causal EoS from those associated with a particular choice of EoS prior, providing a transparent benchmark for interpreting neutron-star observations.

</details>


### [115] [Supernovae with the Square Kilometre Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25223)
*Poonam Chandra,Yuhei Iwata,A. J. Nayana,Miguel Pérez-Torres,Kovi Rose*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本章阐述了平方公里阵列（SKA）在研究各类超新星及其环境方面的科学潜力，系统更新并扩展了Perez-Torres等人（2015）的工作，展示了SKA1及其先导设备如何通过高灵敏度、宽视场巡天将射电超新星天体物理学从发现驱动型转变为群体统计驱动型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年时域天文学和射电仪器取得显著进展，亟需更新早期SKA超新星科学潜力评估；当前射电超新星研究仍处于发现驱动阶段，受限于样本量，需要转向大规模统计研究以全面理解超新星物理。

Method: 利用SKA1及其先导设备的高灵敏度、宽视场、共生式巡天能力；对核坍缩超新星进行大规模巡天以发现每年数百例事件；对热核超新星进行深场触发式观测；结合多信使和多波段协同观测（ALMA、ngVLA、CTA、IceCube-Gen2、ULTRASAT）。

Result: 可实现每年数百例核坍缩超新星的发现，提供无尘埃遮蔽的大质量恒星死亡普查；直接测量体积核坍缩超新星率；探测抛射物-环星介质相互作用、激波微观物理及前身星质量损失历史；通过检测或排除单简并系统的瞬时射电辐射来检验Ia型超新星前身星模型。

Conclusion: SKA将把射电超新星天体物理学从发现驱动型转变为群体统计驱动型，使该领域进入大规模人口统计研究的新时代。

Abstract: This chapter presents the science potential of the Square Kilometre Array (SKA) for studying all classes of supernovae and their environments. It substantially updates and extends the earlier work of Perez-Torres et al. (2015), originally published in the 2015 Advancing Astrophysics with the SKA (AASKA14) volume, reflecting the dramatic progress in time-domain astronomy and radio instrumentation over the past decade. We outline how SKA1 and its pathfinders will transform the radio study of core-collapse supernovae (CCSNe) through sensitive, commensal wide-field surveys capable of discovering hundreds of events per year, providing a dust-unbiased census of massive-star deaths and direct measurements of the volumetric CCSN rate. The same data will probe ejecta-circumstellar medium (CSM) interaction, shock microphysics, and progenitor mass-loss histories. Deep, triggered observations of thermonuclear supernovae (SNe Ia) will allow the SKA to test competing progenitor scenarios by detecting -- or definitively excluding -- the prompt radio emission expected from single-degenerate systems. The chapter further explores superluminous supernovae (SLSNe), delayed interaction supernovae and synergies with facilities such as ALMA, ngVLA, CTA, IceCube-Gen2, and ULTRASAT. Collectively, these studies will turn radio supernova astrophysics from a discovery-limited field into one governed by population statistics.

</details>


### [116] [Constraints on Line-of-Sight Acceleration from O1-O4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25304)
*Labani Roy,Alexander H. Nitz*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了致密双星在致密环境中的质心加速度对其引力波信号的多普勒效应，提出了一种新的时域建模方法，并发现目前所有已知双星的视线加速度与零一致，同时指出偏心率和加速度效应存在部分简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密双星在超大质量第三体附近会产生质心加速度，其视线分量会在引力波信号中表现为时变多普勒频移。探测非零视线加速度可揭示双星是否处于活跃星系核盘或核星团等致密环境中。当前缺乏有效的时域建模方法，且现有观测数据对这些加速度的约束能力尚不明确。

Method: 在时域中直接对双星参考系产生的引力波信号施加时变多普勒频移来建模视线加速度。该方法可适用于任意波形模型，包括包含高阶模式、偏心率和进动的模型。对Advanced LIGO和Virgo的O1-O4a观测数据以及后续运行中的部分双星进行了测量。

Result: 所有已知双星的视线加速度与零一致，未发现非零信号。在GW200105等双星中观测到偏心率和视线加速度效应存在部分简并。当前地面观测台对BBH（BNS）源的加速度灵敏度分别约为10^{-2} c/s（10^{-5} c/s）。

Conclusion: 目前引力波观测对致密环境加速的约束能力有限，仅能限制产生高加速度的场景；下一代引力波观测台有望对某些致密环境中预期的加速度水平进行有效约束。

Abstract: A compact binary will experience a center-of-mass (CoM) acceleration in the vicinity of a massive third object. The line-of-sight (LOS) component of this acceleration is imprinted on gravitational waves produced by the compact binary as a time-varying Doppler shift. The observation of a non-zero LOS acceleration may indicate the binary is in a dense environment, such as an active galactic nucleus (AGN) disk or nuclear star cluster, etc. We measure the LOS acceleration of all compact binaries observed through the first part of the fourth observing run (O1-O4a) of Advanced LIGO and Virgo in addition to select binaries from later observing runs. We introduce a new method to model the LOS acceleration by directly applying the time-varying Doppler shift in the time domain to the signal produced in the binary's frame; this method can be applied to any waveform model including those with higher order modes, eccentricity, and precession. We find the LOS acceleration for all known binaries to date is consistent with zero. We find that the effects of eccentricity and LOS acceleration are partially degenerate as observed in binaries such as GW200105. Current ground-based observatories are sensitive enough to only constrain scenarios that produce high accelerations, e.g $\sim 10^{-2~}(10^{-5})~\textrm{c}/s$ for BBH (BNS) sources, however, next-generation observatories may be able to constrain the accelerations expected in some dense environments.

</details>


### [117] [Amortized Simulation-Based Inference of Relativistic Mean-Field Couplings for Neutron-Star Equations of State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25446)
*Prashant Thakur,Tuhin Malik*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出基于模拟的推理框架，利用神经后验估计（NPE）约束中子星状态方程的相对论平均场（RMF）参数。对DDB和RMF-NL两种模型，使用核饱和性质、手征有效场论中子压强和最大质量约束作为条件观测，训练出的NPE在无显著偏差下复现了嵌套采样结果，可在CPU上2.5秒生成3×10⁴后验样本，实现超快速探索性推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统嵌套采样（如PyMultiNest）在约束中子星状态方程微观参数时计算成本高昂，难以快速迭代和探索大参数空间；需要一种能够快速、可复用且无偏的推理方法来加速对RMF模型参数的约束分析。

Method: 采用神经后验估计（NPE）作为模拟推理框架，训练两个代表性RMF参数族（密度依赖的DDB模型和非线性RMF-NL模型）的条件后验分布；以核饱和性质、手征有效场论纯中子物质压强和最大质量约束为条件观测；使用TARP覆盖诊断和PyMultiNest嵌套采样结果进行验证。

Result: NPE后验在模型耦合常数、核物质性质和星体可观测量上均无显著偏差地复现嵌套采样结果；amortized estimator可在CPU上2.5秒生成3×10⁴个后验样本。模拟观测测试中，约束R_{1.4}=12 km和M_max>1.97 M⊙时，DDB给出M_max=2.10^{+0.09}_{-0.07} M⊙、R_max=10.71^{+0.14}_{-0.21} km；RMF-NL给出M_max=2.05^{+0.10}_{-0.06} M⊙、R_max=10.69^{+0.18}_{-0.19} km。

Conclusion: 经验证的NPE模拟RMF模型可安全用于超快速探索性推理，无需为更新数据重新训练；两种模型在固定R_{1.4}时被约束到窄EOS区域，但RMF-NL在高密度下仍略软于DDB，与其略低的最大质量一致。

Abstract: We present a simulation-based inference framework for constraining microscopic relativistic mean-field parameters of neutron-star equations of state. Neural posterior estimation is applied to two representative RMF families, a density-dependent DDB model and a nonlinear RMF-NL model, using nuclear saturation properties, chiral effective-field-theory pure-neutron-matter pressures, and the maximum-mass constraint as conditioning observables. The inferred posteriors are validated against the conventional nested sampler (PyMultiNest) calculations and tested with the TARP coverage diagnostic. For both RMF parametrizations, the neural posterior reproduces the nested-sampling constraints on model couplings, nuclear-matter properties, and neutron-star observables with no significant bias. The amortized estimator generates $3\times 10^{4}$ posterior samples in about $2.5\,\mathrm{s}$ on a CPU, enabling a rapid inference workflow without the need for retraining for updated data. This constitutes a proof of concept that NPE-emulated RMF models, once validated, can be safely used for superfast exploratory inference. As an additional mock-observation test, imposing $R_{1.4}=12\,{\rm km}$ and $M_{\rm max}>1.97\,M_\odot$ leads to consistent predictions for the maximum-mass configuration, with DDB giving $M_{\rm max}=2.10^{+0.09}_{-0.07}\,M_\odot$, $R_{\rm max}=10.71^{+0.14}_{-0.21}\,{\rm km}$ and RMF-NL giving $M_{\rm max}=2.05^{+0.10}_{-0.06}\,M_\odot$, $R_{\rm max}=10.69^{+0.18}_{-0.19}\,{\rm km}$; although fixing $R_{1.4}$ confines both families to a narrow EOS region, RMF-NL remains marginally softer than DDB at high density, consistent with its slightly lower maximum mass.

</details>


### [118] [Accreting Compact Object Binaries with the SKA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25515)
*Aru Beri,Francesco Carotenuto,Rob P. Fender,James C. A. Miller-Jones,Sara Motta,Valeriu Tudose,Jakob van den Eijnden*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了吸积双星系统中喷流的研究现状，强调了从恒星质量到超大质量黑洞的尺度不变性，并展望了SKA望远镜如何通过高灵敏度、高分辨率和大频率覆盖，将喷流研究从已知的黑洞X射线双星扩展到更暗弱的中子星、白矮星以及邻近星系中的高吸积率系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积双星系统提供了研究吸积与喷流过程的时间分辨视角，而喷流与吸积流的耦合机制是理解所有致密天体（从恒星质量到超大质量黑洞）的关键。现有射电设施虽已取得进展，但仍需更强大的观测能力来探测更暗弱的天体群体，从而完整刻画喷流的物理机制及其反馈作用。

Method: 采用比较研究的思路，通过对比不同致密天体（黑洞、中子星、白矮星）的喷流性质，利用质量、自旋、磁场和恒星表面等参数的差异来解构喷流发射的物理条件。SKA将通过其高灵敏度、高角分辨率、宽频覆盖以及表面亮度灵敏度等能力，实现对更暗弱源（如休眠态系统、中子星和白矮星）的探测。

Result: 现有射电设施（包括SKA先导望远镜）已在黑洞X射线双星的喷流研究中取得重要进展。SKA将在此基础上，把研究扩展至更暗弱的吸积中子星和白矮星群体，发现低光度休眠态中的未知系统，并将研究范围扩展至邻近星系，揭示高吸积率系统这一黑洞增长的关键阶段。

Conclusion: SKA将极大推动吸积双星喷流研究，通过其卓越的观测能力，系统性地将喷流研究从已知的黑洞系统扩展到更广泛的中子星、白矮星及河外星系群体，从而更全面地理解喷流发射的物理机制及其在不同尺度下的统一性。

Abstract: Accreting binary systems provide a time-resolved view of the accretion and ejection processes that are seen in all classes of compact objects, from stellar-mass to supermassive. They allow us to study the launching of relativistic jets on human timescales, probing how the jets are coupled to the underlying accretion flow, and providing unique insights that can be extended to supermassive black holes via the well-established mass scale invariance. Comparative studies of jets from different classes of compact objects allow us to determine the impact of mass, spin, magnetic fields, and stellar surfaces on the launching of jets. The jets from black hole X-ray binaries provide probes of the Galactic black hole population, and an important source of feedback to the surrounding interstellar medium. While existing radio facilities (including the SKA precursors) have made great progress in this field in recent years, the SKA will enhance such studies via its high point source and surface brightness sensitivity, high angular resolution, and broad frequency coverage. This will enable the extension of our existing black hole studies to the fainter accreting neutron star and white dwarf population, the detection of previously-undiscovered systems in a low-luminosity quiescent state, and the extension of our studies to nearby galaxies, revealing rare, high-accretion rate systems that probe a key phase of black hole growth.

</details>


### [119] [Compact Objects Revealed by SKA and SKA-VLBI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25543)
*Z. H. Lin,Y. J. Li,C. J. Hao,J. J. Li,Y. W. Dong,D. J. Liu,Y. Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文阐述了SKA/SKA-VLBI设施凭借μJy级灵敏度和μas级位置精度，将在致密天体（黑洞、中子星）研究中实现三大突破：构建黑洞-中子星双星观测目录以推进核物质状态方程和强场相对论效应研究；精确测定致密双星的轨道参数与质量，揭示极端条件下恒星结构与演化机制；识别中等质量黑洞并测量其质量，深化对黑洞形成与演化的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致密天体是连接天文学与基础物理学的关键交叉前沿领域，但目前对其轨道动力学、状态方程及形成演化机制的认识仍十分有限，亟需高灵敏度、高精度观测手段来突破现有研究瓶颈。

Method: 利用SKA/SKA-VLBI的高灵敏度（μJy级）和超高位置精度（μas级）进行直接、精确的致密天体轨道动力学测量。

Result: 预计实现三大突破：建立黑洞-中子星双星观测目录；精确测定致密双星轨道参数与质量；识别并测量中等质量黑洞质量。

Conclusion: SKA/SKA-VLBI将推动致密天体研究，深化对超密核物质状态方程、强场相对论效应、极端条件下恒星结构与演化以及黑洞形成演化规律的认识。

Abstract: Compact objects represent a crucial interdisciplinary frontier between astronomy and fundamental physics.The SKA/SKA-VLBI, with exceptional sensitivity (at $μ$Jy levels) and ultrahigh positional precision (at $μ$as levels), will enable direct, precise measurements of orbital dynamics in compact objects including black holes and neutron stars. This facility is expected to achieve at least three breakthroughs: (1) Developing effective methodologies for detecting and identifying black hole-neutron star binaries to construct observational catalogues, thus advancing investigations into the equation of state of ultra-dense nuclear matter and strong-field relativistic effects; (2) Determining critical parameters such as orbital elements and component masses in compact binaries, yielding insights into stellar structures and evolutionary mechanisms under extreme conditions; (3) Identifying intermediate-mass black holes and measuring their masses to deepen understanding of black hole formation and evolution.

</details>


### [120] [Probing Physical Conditions in Classical and Symbiotic Novae with the Square Kilometre Array Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25609)
*Rocco Lico,Marcello Giroletti,Arnau Aguasca-Cabot,Joris Kersten,Benito Marcote,Ulisse Munari,Miriam M. Nyamai,Timothy J. O'Brien,Zsolt Paragi,Iris de Ruiter,Kirill Sokolovsky,David R. A. Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了经典新星和共生新星（由白矮星与伴星组成的双星系统）中的射电和γ射线辐射机制，强调了SKAO（特别是SKA-Mid）在未来多频率、多历元、多尺度观测中的关键作用，能够定期监测银河系新星，探测热核爆炸后的物理演化过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在展示通过对经典新星和共生新星的多波段、多尺度射电观测，如何揭示热核爆炸后的物理条件演化，并突出SKAO在未来研究中的巨大潜力。

Method: 采用多频率、多历元、多尺度干涉观测方法，结合射电和γ射线数据，分析热核爆炸后电离气体和相对论性粒子群体的演化；利用SKAO（尤其是SKA-Mid）的VLBI能力和高灵敏度进行定期监测和致密激波区成像。

Result: SKAO能够实现对银河系经典新星每年多次、共生新星每数年一次的定期监测；其VLBI能力可探测致密激波区，高灵敏度可表征双星宁静期的辐射特征。

Conclusion: SKAO将极大推动新星研究，通过宽参数空间（伴星类型、白矮星质量、吸积机制、周围环境）的系统观测，深化对双星演化、吸积、物质抛射、喷流形成和热核燃烧等关键天体物理过程的理解。

Abstract: Cataclysmic variables and symbiotic stars are interacting binary systems in which a hot white dwarf (WD) orbits a companion main-sequence or red giant star, respectively. Accumulation of hydrogen-rich material on the WD surface may trigger re-ignition of thermonuclear reactions that, under degenerate conditions, lead to an explosive ejection of the accreted layer mixed with WD material. These explosions, known as classical novae, provide opportunities to study key astrophysical processes such as binary evolution, accretion, ionisation of circumstellar material, mass ejection, jet formation, and thermonuclear burning. Radio emission in these systems arises from both thermal and non-thermal processes, which manifest differently in classical and symbiotic novae. $γ$-ray emission has also been detected in several cases, and recent progress, driven by coordinated radio and multiwavelength observations, has greatly advanced our understanding of both types of novae. Multi-frequency, multi-epoch, and multi-scale interferometric observations are powerful probes of the evolving physical conditions following thermonuclear explosions, revealing information from both ionised and relativistic particle populations. The SKAO, particularly its SKA-Mid component, will enable regular monitoring of Galactic novae, multiple times per year for classical novae and every few years for symbiotic systems. It will explore a wide range of conditions, including companion types, WD masses, accretion regimes, and surrounding environments. The VLBI capabilities of the SKAO will target compact shocked regions, while its exceptional sensitivity will also allow characterisation of emission during quiescent phases of the binaries.

</details>


### [121] [Observation of A Solar Like Magnetic Reconnection Event in an AGN Corona with XRISM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25636)
*Gal Vardi,Ehud Behar,Liyi Gu,Jelle Kaastra,Matteo Guainazzi,Missagh Mehdipour,Keigo Fukumura,Jon Miller,Ari Laor,Erin Kara,Megan E. Eckart,Misaki Mizumoto,Christos Panagiotou,Chen Li,Ogawa Shoji,Matilde Signorini*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文首次在活动星系核（AGN）冕中观测到磁重联耀斑的直接证据。通过XRISM/Xtend和XMM-Newton/EPIC-PN对NGC 3783的观测，发现软硬X射线的时间演化差异，并探测到超快外流（UFO）。耀斑表现出Neupert效应——太阳耀斑的典型特征，表明其由磁重联驱动，UFO的作用类似于日冕物质抛射（CME）。研究限制磁圈高度上限为30 R_g，并根据UFO特性推导出耀斑湮灭磁场>1.3×10^4 G，重联瞬间磁场约500 G。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AGN冕与恒星冕在加热机制上可能相似，但磁重联加热AGN冕从未被直接观测到。本文旨在提供首个观测证据，证明AGN冕中确实存在磁重联耀斑。

Method: 利用XRISM/Xtend和XMM-Newton/EPIC-PN对NGC 3783进行X射线观测，分析软硬X射线（以2.0 keV为界）的时间演化特征，检测超快外流（UFO），并与太阳耀斑的Neupert效应进行类比分析，结合UFO参数对磁场强度进行能量学和动力学约束。

Result: 1. 在NGC 3783中观测到磁重联耀斑，软硬X射线表现出不同的时间演化；2. 耀斑期间探测到UFO；3. 观测到Neupert效应，确认磁重联为能量来源；4. 磁圈高度上限为30 R_g；5. 湮灭磁场>1.3×10^4 G，重联瞬间磁场≈500 G。

Conclusion: AGN冕的加热机制确实类似于太阳，由磁重联驱动，UFO相当于日冕物质抛射。这是首次直接观测证据表明AGN冕中存在磁重联耀斑，为理解AGN能量释放过程提供了新视角。

Abstract: The X-ray source in AGN is commonly referred to as the corona by analogy to stellar coronae. The similarities between the two suggest that the heating mechanism of AGN coronae is magnetic reconnection -- as in cool stars -- but this has not yet been directly observed. This work presents the first observational evidence for a magnetic reconnection flare in an AGN corona. We report on a flare in NGC 3783, which was observed with XRISM/Xtend and XMM-Newton/EPIC-PN exhibiting distinct temporal evolution in soft ($<2.0\,$keV) and hard ($>2.0\,$keV) X-rays. An Ultra-Fast Outflow (UFO) was detected during the event. The flare features the Neupert effect -- a temporal signature widely observed in the Sun, which shows that the flare is powered by magnetic reconnection, with the UFO playing a role analogous to a solar Coronal Mass Ejection (CME). We derive an upper limit of $30 \, R_g$ on the height of the magnetic loop from which the flare originates. Using the UFO's measured properties to characterize the magnetic field, we obtain $B > 1.3 \times 10^4\,$G for the field annihilated during the flare from total energy considerations, and $B \approx 500\,$G for the momentary magnetic field during reconnection from a dynamical consideration.

</details>


### [122] [A magnetically-supported disk-corona model for Changing-Look AGN transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25654)
*Marios Kouzis,Agata Różańska,Debora Lančová,Bożena Czerny,Dominik Gronkiewicz*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出磁化盘-冕模型成功解释变脸活动星系核（CLAGN）在数月至数年时间尺度上的光谱与光度跃迁现象。通过diskvert代码计算热-粘滞S曲线和热时标，发现强磁场将S曲线拐点压低至爱丁顿比~0.01-0.03，并引入新的高光度稳定分支，限环时标在M_BH=10^7-10^9 M⊙范围内落入数月到数年区间。对五个CLAGN样本（Mkn 590等）的检验表明，只有内盘强磁化时模型才能同时再现观测的爱丁顿比率和跃迁持续时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLAGN在数月到数年时间尺度上发生剧烈光谱和光度跃迁，远小于标准α盘在光学/UV连续谱产生半径处的粘性时标，现有理论难以解释这种快速转变。因此需要发展新的理论模型来解释CLAGN的快速跃迁现象。

Method: 使用diskvert代码（求解同时考虑气体、辐射和磁压支持的稳态垂直结构，包含自洽暖冕），构建热-粘滞S曲线，计算积分热时标和前沿传播时标。计算不同黑洞质量、爱丁顿比率、磁粘滞系数和盘半径的大规模模型网格。

Result: 磁化盘将S曲线拐点压低至爱丁顿比~0.01-0.03，并引入新的高光度稳定分支；限环时标在M_BH=10^7-10^9 M⊙时落入数月到数年范围；对五个CLAGN样本的检验表明，仅当内盘强磁化时模型能同时再现观测的爱丁顿比率和跃迁持续时间。

Conclusion: 磁化盘-冕模型能解释CLAGN的快速跃迁现象，强磁场是同时满足观测爱丁顿比率和跃迁时标的关键因素，其中Mkn 590的紧密约束的跃迁爱丁顿比率与小半径处高磁化盘-冕流相匹配。

Abstract: Changing-Look Active Galactic Nuclei (CLAGN) undergo dramatic spectral and luminosity transitions on timescales of months to a few years -- orders of magnitude shorter than the viscous timescale of a standard $α$-disk at the radii where the optical/UV continuum is generated, for typical supermassive black hole masses. We show that a magnetically supported disk-corona model reproduces \emph{both} the observed Eddington ratio at which changing event occurs and the observed transition duration. Using the \texttt{diskvert} code, which solves the steady vertical structure under simultaneous gas, radiation and magnetic pressure support with a self-consistent warm corona, we (i) construct thermal-viscous S-curves, and (ii) calculate the integrated thermal timescale together with the front propagation timescale. We compute a large grid of models of different black hole masses, Eddington ratios, magnetic viscosities, and disk radii, showing that magnetized disks push the S-curve knee down to an Eddington ratio of $ \approx 0.01-0.03$, and introduce a new stable branch of high luminosity solutions, while the limit-cycle timescale enters the months-to-years range for $M_\mathrm{BH} = 10^{7}-10^{9}\,\mathrm{M_\odot}$. Confronted with a sample of five CLAGN (Mkn 590, NGC 1566, IRAS 23226$-$3843, Mkn 1018, NGC 2617), the model jointly reproduces the empirical Eddington rates and the observed event durations only when the inner disk is strongly magnetized. The case of Mkn 590 is especially constraining: the recent tightly-determined transition Eddington ratio is matched by a highly magnetized disk-corona flow at small radii.

</details>


### [123] [Multi-Band Optical Variability of Blazar 1ES 2344+514 on Diverse Time-Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25741)
*Aykut Özdönmez*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对耀变体1ES 2344+514在光学波段的多波段观测（2022-2024年）和ZTF巡天数据（2018-2023年）分析，发现该源具有小幅度的短期和长期光变（~0.7 mag和0.4 mag），日内无分钟级光变，存在中等程度的“越亮越红”趋势，并在ZTF光变曲线中探测到约1.02年、1.3年和2.85年的准周期振荡信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本研究旨在通过多波段、多时间尺度的观测，全面表征耀变体1ES 2344+514的光学波段流量变化特性，以揭示其复杂的辐射机制和内在物理过程。

Method: 使用了2022-2024年多波段观测数据和2018-2023年ZTF巡天数据；采用功率增强F检验和嵌套方差分析（ANOVA）进行日内光变统计检验；通过多波段颜色行为分析研究颜色变化趋势；利用WWZ和LS方法搜索准周期振荡信号。

Result: 该耀变体具有小幅度的短期和长期光变（去宿主星等~0.7 mag，含宿主星等~0.4 mag）；日内无分钟级光变；日内存在中等程度的“越亮越红”趋势，长期无显著颜色变化；ZTF光变曲线间强相关无时延，但光学波段间无显著相关；探测到约1.02年、1.3年和2.85年的QPO信号。

Conclusion: 耀变体1ES 2344+514表现出复杂的光变特性，其辐射机制需要未来更多观测数据来进一步阐明。

Abstract: This study presents the results of multi-band observations from 2022 to 2024 and Zwicky Transient Facility (ZTF) observations from 2018 to 2023, examining the flux variability of the blazar 1ES 2344+514 on diverse time-scales in the optical bands. The blazar has mild short-term variability (STV) and long-term variability (LTV), with small amplitudes of $\sim0.7$ mag and 0.4 mag for the host subtracted- and included-light curves, respectively. The power-enhanced F-test and the nested Analysis of Variance (ANOVA) statistical tests of the six intra-day light curves show that the blazar has no minute-scale variability. The multiband color behavior analysis revealed a moderate redder-when-brighter (RWB) trend on intra-day time scales, while the LTV shows no detectable color behavior. We found a strong correlation between the ZTF optical light curves without any time lag, but no detectable correlations for the optical band emissions. From our periodicity searches using WWZ and LS methods, three significant quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) signals in the ZTF light curves are found at about 1.02, 1.3, and 2.85 years. The observational results indicate that the blazar 1ES 2344+514 has a complex variability while emphasizing the need for future observations to unravel its underlying mechanisms.

</details>


### [124] [Study for curvature radiation and magnetic pair creation process on polar-cap region of magnetic white dwarf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25806)
*Bingyan Wang,Jumpei Takata*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了快速旋转、强磁化白矮星极冠加速区的粒子加速、辐射、对产生及同步辐射过程。采用空间电荷限制流模型，通过一维数值计算和解析估计，探讨自旋周期和表面磁场的影响，并比较纯偶极场与偶极-四极复合场的作用。结果表明，四极分量能增强加速场并提高辐射效率；对产生仅发生在周期P≲100秒且偶极场B≲10^10高斯条件下，说明已知磁白矮星难以维持对产生。讨论了模型对旋转驱动活动和长周期射电暂现源的解释意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速旋转、强磁化白矮星可能像中子星脉冲星一样产生旋转驱动的活动现象，但目前对这类系统中粒子加速和辐射机制的理解尚不充分，需要系统研究。

Method: 基于空间电荷限制流模型，采用一维数值计算和解析估计相结合的方法，分别考虑纯偶极场和偶极-四极复合场两种磁场位形，系统分析极冠区粒子加速、辐射和对产生过程。

Result: 四极分量减小了磁力线曲率半径，显著增强加速场，从而提升辐射和对产生的效率。对产生仅能发生在P≲100 s且B_*,d≲10^10 G的参数范围内，表明已知磁白矮星难以维持对产生。

Conclusion: 该模型为快速旋转孤立磁白矮星的旋转驱动活动提供了理论框架，并可用于解释长周期射电暂现源的可能白矮星起源。

Abstract: Rapidly rotating, strongly magnetized white dwarfs (WDs) have been proposed as potential sites of rotation-powered activity analogous to that of a neutron star pulsar. In this study, we investigate particle acceleration, radiation processes, pair creation and resulting synchrotron radiation in the polar cap acceleration region.
  Within the framework of the space-charge-limited flow model, we examine how these processes depend on the spin period and surface magnetic field using both one-dimensional numerical calculations and analytical estimates.
  To explore the impact of the magnetic field geometry on the accelerating process, we consider both a pure dipole field and a combination of dipole and quadrupole fields.
  The inclusion of a quadrupole component reduces the curvature radius of the magnetic field lines, and significantly enhances the accelerating field, leading to more efficient radiation and pair creation processes. Using this framework, we evaluate the WD death line with a more consistent treatment of the relevant physical processes than the previous studies.
  We find that the pair creation process can occur for spin periods $P\lesssim100$~s, when the dipole field strength $B_{*,d}\lesssim10^{10}$~G, indicating that pair creation is difficult to sustain in currently known magnetic WDs. We discuss the implications of our model for rotation-powered activity in rapidly spinning isolated magnetic WDs and for the possible WD interpretation of long-period radio transients.

</details>


### [125] [Hybrid radio and particle detection of air showers: potential for ultra-high-energy photon identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25833)
*Paul Minodier,Kaoru Takahashi,Kumiko Kotera,Takashi Sako*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合无线电天线和闪烁体探测器阵列对超高能光子的探测能力，通过分析倾斜大气簇射的无线电信号和缪子信号差异，实现对宇宙射线与光子的有效区分。以GRANDProto300布局为案例，证明该混合阵列在0.3-3 EeV能量范围内可设置具有竞争力的积分光子通量上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无线电探测器配合闪烁体是否能够成为具有竞争力的超高能光子探测器，以拓展对UHE光子的探测能力。

Method: 利用两个关键观测量——无线电信号的总均方根值和闪烁体记录的总能量沉积——在混合无线电天线和闪烁体阵列中区分宇宙射线和光子引发的倾斜簇射，并以GRANDProto300布局为案例进行估计。

Result: 该混合阵列在0.3-3 EeV能量范围内能够设置具有竞争力的积分光子通量上限。

Conclusion: 混合无线电天线与闪烁体阵列可有效分离宇宙射线和光子引发的倾斜簇射，有望成为有竞争力的UHE光子探测器。

Abstract: The autonomous radio-detection of extensive air showers initiated by ultra-high-energy (UHE) particles arriving with very inclined zenith angles has seen significant advancements in recent years, with several large-scale surface arrays planned and prototypes already in operation. In this work, we examine whether these radio detectors, supplemented by scintillators, could serve as competitive UHE photon detectors. Indeed, for inclined showers, radio emissions can be detected by antennas for both cosmic-ray and photon primaries, while the muon-rich signatures of the former would typically trigger the scintillators. Using two key observables -- the total root mean square of the radio signal and the total energy deposit recorded in the scintillators -- we show that effective separation between the two types of showers could be achieved in a hybrid radio antenna and scintillator setup. As a case study, we apply this method to a hypothetical hybrid array of radio antennas, complemented by Telescope Array-like scintillators, with the layout of the prototype of the Giant Radio Array for Neutrino Detection (GRAND), GRANDProto300. Our estimates show that such a hybrid array could set competitive upper limits on the integral photon flux in the energy range of approximately 0.3 to 3 EeV.

</details>

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文基于2017年4月事件视界望远镜（EHT）对耀变体3C 279的观测及同期多波段数据，分析了其核心射电增亮、UV-光学耀发、伽马射线活动及X射线低态等特征，并利用湍流极端多区（TEMZ）模型进行现象学建模，提出湍流等离子体细胞被静止锥形激波压缩的喷流情景，同时提供了一套公开的多波段参考数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究耀变体3C 279核心喷流的物理过程及宽带变异的起源，特别是伽马射线发射机制。

Method: 分析EHT 230 GHz观测、多波段光变曲线，构建准同时多波段能谱，并采用湍流极端多区（TEMZ）模型进行现象学建模。

Result: EHT显示核心射电流量在4月5日至11日间明显增加；长波射电在4月中旬出现核心流量与偏振增强，与超光速节点抛射一致；3月下旬发生创纪录的UV-光学耀发伴随强偏振变异；伽马射线活动在EHT观测期结束前衰减；X射线处于低态，无甚高能（VHE）发射。

Conclusion: TEMZ模型支持湍流等离子体细胞被静止锥形激波压缩的喷流解释，但磁重联或移动激波等替代机制仍有可能。该工作为理解3C 279喷流及伽马射线发射起源提供了新见解，并贡献了公开的多波段数据集。

Abstract: The archetypal blazar 3C 279 hosts a prominent relativistic jet and exhibits strong broadband variability across the electromagnetic spectrum. In April 2017, the Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) observed 3C 279, alongside one of the most extensive quasi-simultaneous multiwavelength (MWL) campaigns ever conducted. With the aim of investigating the physical processes governing 3C 279, we analyzed individual observations and multiband light curves, and constructed a new quasi-simultaneous MWL spectrum. We also performed phenomenological modeling using the turbulent extreme multi-zone (TEMZ) model to constrain the fundamental physical properties of the source. The EHT observations reveal a clear flux increase in the innermost core between April 5 and 11, 2017. Over a broader timescale, radio measurements at longer wavelengths show concurrent enhancements in core flux and polarization around mid-April, coinciding with the ejection of a superluminal knot. Record UV-optical flares with strong polarization variability occurred in late March, followed by gamma-ray activity that declined before the end of the EHT observing period. During this interval, the source remained in a low X-ray state and showed no detectable VHE emission. The TEMZ modeling suggests that the broadband spectrum and variability of 3C 279 can be explained within a jet scenario in which turbulent plasma cells are compressed by a stationary conical shock. However, alternative interpretations, such as magnetic reconnection or a moving shock-in-jet event, remain plausible. This coordinated MWL campaign advances our understanding of the origin of jet and gamma-ray emission in 3C 279, while also providing a comprehensive publicly available dataset that will serve as a valuable reference for future studies.

</details>


### [127] [Two years of shock interaction tracing three phases of evolution: the explosion of a Type IIn supernova, SN 2019vxm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25896)
*Gitika Rameshan,Rishabh Singh Teja,D. K. Sahu,G. C. Anupama,Masayuki Yamanaka,Keiichi Maeda,Tatsuya Nakaoka,Sota Goto,Brajesh Kumar,Avinash Singh,Miho Kawabata,Koji S. Kawabata,Kenta Taguchi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: SN 2019vxm是一颗长寿命相互作用超新星，光变曲线上升缓慢（R波段约45.9天），峰值绝对星等约-20.3 mag，总辐射光度5×10^50 erg。通过光变曲线模型估算CSM质量为3-8 M_sun，抛射物质量至少约3.88 M_sun。早期以相互作用主导，后期出现尘埃消光特征（温度~1500 K，距离~4×10^16 cm），谱线呈现不对称轮廓且红翼被强烈抑制，晚期未出现星云谱线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对长寿命相互作用超新星SN 2019vxm进行多波段测光和光学光谱观测，覆盖爆发后两年以上，以揭示其独特的演化特征和物理机制。

Method: 多波段测光观测、光学光谱观测、光变曲线模型拟合、与理论模型对比分析。

Result: SN 2019vxm光变曲线上升缓慢（R波段~45.9天），峰值绝对星等~-20.3 mag；总辐射光度5×10^50 erg；CSM质量3-8 M_sun；抛射物质量至少3.88 M_sun；晚期红外增亮；谱线不对称且红翼被抑制；存在温度~1500 K、距离~4×10^16 cm的尘埃；晚期无星云谱线。

Conclusion: SN 2019vxm是一颗能量较高的长寿命相互作用超新星，其演化经历了从相互作用主导到尘埃消光主导的转变，晚期尘埃形成导致光学辐射减弱和红外增亮，且抛射物被尘埃遮蔽或密度较高。

Abstract: We present multi-wavelength photometric and optical spectroscopic observations of the long-lived interacting supernova SN 2019vxm, spanning more than two years after the explosion. SN 2019vxm is a slowly rising (rise time ~ 45.9 days in the R-band), slowly declining supernova reaching an R-band peak absolute magnitude of ~-20.3 mag. The SN light curve post-maximum shows a shallow decline, followed by a secondary, steeper decline in the optical (0.01 mag/day), with late-time IR brightening. The total radiated luminosity is 5x10^50 erg, placing it among the energetic class of its type. We estimated a CSM mass of 3-8 M_sun through light-curve modeling (independent of the CSM density profile) and by comparison with theoretical models. We estimate a minimum ejecta mass of ~ 3.88 M_sun from the broad H-alpha component, consistent with the ejecta mass obtained from the light curve models. The solely interaction-dominated initial epochs are later accompanied by photon-scattering signatures, leading to asymmetric line profiles with symmetric wings. The late phase, characterized by enhanced brightness at longer wavelengths and a stronger asymmetric line profile with the red side flux strongly suppressed, indicates the influence of pre-existing or newly formed dust with temperatures ~ 1500 K at ~4x10^16 cm. Even in the late phases, no nebular lines are present in the spectra, indicating dense or obscured ejecta.

</details>


### [128] [UsingSKA-Low to Detect PeV Gamma-rays from Galactic Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25932)
*Anna Nelles,Philipp Laub,Haoning He,Felix Schlüter,Sjoerd Bouma,Justin Bray,Stijn Buitink,Arthur Corstanje,Vital De Henau,Edwin Dickinson,Brian Hare,Jörg Hörandel,Tim Huege,Clancy James,Xingyu Li,Hermann-Josef Mathes,Katharine Mulrey,Subhadip Saha,Olaf Scholten,Ralph Spencer,Christopher Sterpka,Karen Terveer,Satyendra Thoudam,Gia Trinh,Paulina Turekova,Darko Veberic,Keito Watanabe,Hiroaki Yamamoto,Chao Zhang,Pengfei Zhang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用SKA-Low射电望远镜阵列探测PeV能量的伽马射线空气簇射，以此识别银河系中的PeVatrons（PeV宇宙线源），这将是首次通过射电辐射探测伽马射线空气簇射，为理解银河系宇宙线加速器提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PeVatrons是银河系中发射超过PeV（10^15 eV）能量宇宙线的天体物理源，但其性质尚不明确。目前仅有少数候选源勉强达到PeV能量，亟需新的探测手段来识别和研究这些高能粒子加速器。

Method: 利用SKA-Low射电望远镜阵列的大规模天线数量优势，降低空气簇射的射电探测能量阈值（目前已被证明在50 PeV），同时凭借其足够大的核心面积提供显著的等效面积来测量PeV通量。

Result: SKA-Low有望将射电探测能量阈值降低到PeV能区，从而实现对PeVatrons的探测，并首次通过射电辐射方式探测到伽马射线空气簇射。

Conclusion: SKA-Low为识别银河系PeVatrons提供了一种新颖的观测角度，并开辟了射电探测伽马射线空气簇射这一全新领域。

Abstract: Detecting so called PeVatrons is considered one of the prime goals of $γ$-ray astronomy. PeVatrons are astrophysical objects in the Galaxy that are sources of cosmic rays exceeding PeV ($10^{15}$ eV) energies, the highest in our Galaxy. Their nature is unknown as of now, with some candidates reaching barely above PeV energies just having been identified. Serendipitously, the energy threshold of air shower detection using radio emission, has been proven at 50 PeV. There is a case to be made that SKA-Low with its unprecedented number of antennas, can reach lower in energy, while the size of the core is sufficiently large provide a significant effective area to measure PeV fluxes. While this promises a novel angle towards understanding the cosmic ray accelerators in our Galaxy, it also would be the first detection of $γ$-ray air showers using radio emission.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Hodge Spectral Surrogates for Topology-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25194)
*Satoshi Kanno,Yoshi-aki Shimada*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hodge-谱松弛的微分可微框架，用于拓扑约束优化，通过构造Hodge-Laplacian型谱松弛来处理软图和软团复形，利用零模和近零模表征同调信息，并定义了可微拓扑目标函数，在点云和团复形上相比持久同调损失具有更好的梯度分布和几何感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接控制Betti数和持久同调是困难且不可微的，因为它们是离散组合对象，无法直接用于基于梯度的优化框架。

Method: 基于Hodge-谱松弛方法，构造软图和软团复形的Hodge-Laplacian型谱算子；在硬极限下恢复普通Hodge Laplacian，软极限下作为可微低频谱代理；使用热滤波器、预解滤波器和多项式Laplacian矩定义可微拓扑目标。

Result: 在点云上，Hodge谱滤波损失比持久同调损失产生更空间分布的梯度、更平滑的尺度归一化行为以及几何感知的更新方向；在团复形上，Laplacian矩可控制归一化第一Betti型量。

Conclusion: 该框架为拓扑约束优化提供了可微的替代方案，并讨论了与迹估计、多项式谱方法及量子迹估计的联系。

Abstract: Topological information is widely used in data analysis, network design, and machine learning, and topological constraints naturally arise when optimizing or generating objects with prescribed homological structure. However, directly controlling Betti numbers and persistent homology is difficult because they are discrete and combinatorial. We propose a differentiable framework for topology-constrained optimization based on Hodge-spectral relaxations of homological constraints and low-pass spectral filters. From soft graphs and soft clique complexes, we construct Hodge-Laplacian-type spectral relaxations that unify graph clique complexes and Vietoris--Rips filtrations of point clouds. In the hard limit, the penalty-regularized ambient operator recovers the ordinary Hodge Laplacian on the active subcomplex, while in the soft regime it serves as a differentiable low-frequency spectral surrogate. Homological information is represented by zero and near-zero modes, and differentiable topological objectives are defined using heat filters, resolvent filters, and polynomial Laplacian moments. For point clouds, we show that the proposed Hodge spectral-filter losses yield more spatially distributed gradients, smoother scale-normalized behavior under persistence-pairing changes, and geometry-aware update directions than persistent-homology-based losses. For graph clique complexes, Laplacian moments control normalized first-Betti-type quantities and can be combined with ordinary graph-feature objectives. We also discuss connections to trace-based normalized Betti-number estimation, polynomial spectral methods, and possible quantum trace estimation.

</details>


### [130] [Profinite Borel completeness and smooth Artin motives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25943)
*Yorick Fuhrmann*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文分为两部分：第一部分重新审视有限群的Borel完全等变谱的∞-范畴，引入带系数的版本，并推广到profinite群，区分了逐点Borel完全性与超完备化；第二部分研究光滑Artin动机的变体，在Nisnevich情形将其等同于Bredon上同调谱上的模，在étale情形揭示profinite Borel完全性的两种概念差异对应于有限étale方案上的étale层与超层的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Mathew-Naumann-Noel关于有限群Borel完全等变谱的∞-范畴理论到带系数情形和profinite群，并澄清两种不同完备性概念；将光滑Artin动机理论纳入这一框架，建立与Bredon上同调谱模的等价关系，阐明étale情形中层与超层的差异。

Method: 采用∞-范畴理论方法，重新审视和推广Borel完全性概念，将其应用于profinite群；通过识别光滑Artin动机与Bredon上同调谱模的等价性，以及比较étale层与超层的差异来建立联系。

Result: 在Nisnevich情形，光滑Artin动机等价于étale基本群的Bredon上同调谱上的模；在étale情形，profinite Borel完全性的两种概念差异恰好对应于有限étale方案上étale层与超层的差异。

Conclusion: 建立了Borel等变谱理论与光滑Artin动机理论的深刻联系，明确了profinite群情形下不同完备性概念的几何意义，为后续研究提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: The purpose of this paper is twofold. In the first part, we revisit the description of the $\infty$-category of Borel complete equivariant spectra for a finite group given by Mathew-Naumann-Noel, introduce a version with coefficients, and then consider Borel equivariance for profinite groups. Here we identify two generally differing notions: levelwise Borel completeness and the hypercompletion thereof. In the second part, we study variants of smooth Artin motives, which are subcategories of the $\infty$-categories of effective Nisnevich and étale Voevodsky motives over a base scheme $S$ that are controlled by the étale fundamental group $π_1^{\mathrm{ét}}(S)$. In the Nisnevich case, we extend a theorem of Voevodsky and identify smooth Artin motives with modules over the Bredon cohomology spectrum for the profinite group $π_1^{\mathrm{ét}}(S)$. In the étale case, we show that the difference between our two notions of profinite Borel completeness is precisely the difference between étale sheaves and hypersheaves on finite étale schemes.

</details>
